### Page 2

足元の経済・金融状況と注目点
1
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
2026. 11.17
米国では物価高で実質賃金が伸び悩む一方、
カード延滞率に改善の兆し。NY連銀によれば、
延滞率の高止まりはコロナ期の与信拡大が影響
しており、今後も緩やかな改善が続くとの見立て
米家計のクレジットカード延滞率に改善の兆し
米国
10月の消費者物価は市場予想を上回る結果。生産
者物価も高止まり。市場は12月利上げを織り込み。
一方で、金利上昇を背景に中古住宅販売は大幅減。
根強いインフレ圧力と住宅市場の弱さが鮮明に
欧州
ユーロ圏の7～9月期GDPは前期から減速。ドイツ
は小幅プラスも、フランスはマイナス、スペインは堅
調と主要国でばらつき。ユーロ圏全体では雇用の伸
びも横ばいとなっており、景気回復力の鈍さを確認
中国
10月の輸出は高い伸びを維持。半導体や自動車な
どがけん引。一方で輸入も大幅増。生産は輸出堅調
で底堅く推移も、投資は下落幅が拡大し、消費も力
強さ欠き低迷。消費者物価も前月から伸びが鈍化

日本
9月の実質消費支出は10カ月連続のマイナス。景気
ウォッチャー調査も6カ月ぶりに悪化し、家計部門の
弱さを示唆する内容。国内企業物価は原油高などを
受けて再び加速。今後消費者物価へ波及する公算
注目点①
日本の消費は所得の伸びを下回る緩慢な回復。
一方、大型M&Aなどから企業の資金需要は旺盛
で、銀行貸出は高い伸びを維持。預金獲得競争
が激化するなか、安定的な預金調達が重要に
日本の家計は消費に慎重、企業は借入に積極的
注目点②

（年/月）
米国：物価高で実質賃金は伸び悩むも、クレジットカード延滞率に改善の兆し
 2026年10月のコアCPI（食品・エネルギーを除く）は前月比＋0.3％と市場予想（同＋0.2％）を上回る結果
―3カ月移動平均では2025年1月以来の高水準。財・サービス価格ともに上昇が続きインフレ圧力の根強さを確認
― 名目賃金の増加が物価上昇に相殺され、実質平均時給は前月比横ばい。家計の購買力は伸び悩み
 一方、クレジットカードの90日以上延滞への新規移行率は高水準ながらも小幅に低下
―7～9月期は6.87％と前年同期の7.05％を下回り、家計の信用状況には改善の兆し
3
（出所）米国労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

コアCPI クレジットカード債務の新規90日以上延滞率
（出所）ニューヨーク連銀より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
物価上昇が継続 延滞率は高水準も小幅低下 信用状況悪化に一服感
注目点①米家計のクレジットカード延滞率に改善の兆し

米国：カード延滞率の高さは足元の所得環境だけではなく、過去の与信拡大の影響を反映
 NY連銀によれば、コロナ禍には信用力の低い借り手へのカード発行が拡大
― 全体の信用スコアが改善する中、新規発行時の信用スコア中央値は低下。リスクの高い層への与信を増やした格好
 当時発行されたカードがその後の延滞率上昇に大きく寄与
― ただし、コロナ期の与信拡大による影響は徐々にはく落。加えて、2023年以降は新規発行時の信用力が改善しており、
雇用・所得環境が大きく悪化しなければ、今後も延滞率の緩やかな改善が続く公算
4
クレジットカードの信用スコア

日本：個人消費は所得の伸びを下回る緩慢な拡大。先行き不安が回復の重石に
5
 個人消費は拡大基調を維持も、所得の伸びを下回る緩慢な拡大
―9月の消費活動指数は前年比＋0.8％と拡大基調を維持したものの、耐久財特需のはく落などから増勢は鈍化
― 実質雇用者報酬が着実に増加する一方、消費の伸びは所得を下回る状況が継続
 先行き不安も家計の慎重な支出姿勢に影響
― 消費者マインドは食品やエネルギーなど身近な消費財の価格上昇に対する懸念から低調に推移
― 中東情勢を巡る不透明感もあり、当面の消費は緩慢な拡大にとどまる見通し

日本：大型M&Aなどもあり銀行貸出は高い伸び。金利上昇下でも企業の資金需要は底堅く推移
6
 企業の資金需要は旺盛。銀行貸出は高い伸びを維持
―10月の貸出残高（銀行・信用金庫計）は前年比＋5.2％と都市銀行を中心に高い伸び。とりわけ、大企業の資金需要が
旺盛。中堅・中小企業の資金需要にも底堅さ。活発な企業買収も資金需要の追い風に
 金利上昇下でも当面の資金需要は底堅い見通し
― 日銀の利上げに伴い新規貸出金利は上昇も、投資需要などを背景に企業の借入需要は底堅く推移する見込み
― なお、地域銀行を中心に預貸率が上昇。メガバンクを含め預金獲得競争が激化するなか、安定的な預金調達が重要に

（電力価格、2026年1～8月累積、前年同期比、％）
米国：民主党勝利によるデータセンター規制の強化は電力価格の上昇を抑制する可能性
 米国では家庭向け電力価格が上昇。背景に送配電設備の更新・増強や異常気象
― 足元では、データセンター（DC）稼働数と電力価格の上昇率に明確な関係はみられず
 一方、DC増設に伴う将来の電気料金負担増や水資源への影響を懸念し、規制強化を求める動きが拡大
― 中間選挙では、DC規制の強化を掲げる民主党知事候補が各州で勝利。とりわけ、DCの集積が進むテキサス・オハイ
オ・ジョージアでは、長年共和党が知事職を握ってきたものの、民主党候補が勝利する結果。今後、DC開発を巡る規制
が強化され、家計への費用転嫁が抑えられれば、中長期的な電力価格の上昇ペースは緩和される可能性
7
（注） 青色はオハイオ州、テキサス州、ジョージア州

州別家庭向け電力価格と稼働中のDC数 民主党知事候補が勝利した3州のDC規制方針

主な方針州・当選者
条件を満たさないDCの建設を停止。 事業者
に電気・ガス・水道の費用を全額負担させ、
労働組合に加入する労働者による建設、情
報公開、環境基準の遵守などを義務付ける
方針
オハイオ
エイミー・ア
クトン氏
新規DCの承認を一時停止。 住民が建設の
是非に関与できる法律が整うまで、新規案件
を認めない。DC向けの税優遇を廃止し、送
電網の増強費用などを一般家庭に転嫁させ
ない方針
テキサス
ジーナ・ヒノ
ホサ氏
州全体で新規DC建設を一時停止。 電気料
金や水資源への影響を調査し、建設ルール
を整備。DC向けの税優遇を廃止し、電力設
備の整備費用を事業者に負担させる方針

欧州：ユーロ圏経済はプラス成長継続。一方、フランスでは不安定な政治情勢が投資の重石に
 ユーロ圏全体の7～9月期GDPは潜在成長率（約＋1.2％）を上回る伸びを維持
― アイルランドを除くと年率＋0.8％成長。スペインは内需主導で高成長維持も、フランスがマイナス転化する等国ごとに濃淡
 フランスの不安定な政治情勢が景気に暗雲。設備投資の下押し要因に
― フランスでは不安定な政治情勢が経済の不確実性を高めるなか、民間設備投資の足踏みが続く
― 同国の10月企業景況感は前月から大幅に悪化し、縮小圏に転落。先行きも、政治・財政の不透明感の長期化や金融環境
の引き締まりを背景に、一部業種（情報通信サービス等）を除き低調に推移する見込み
8

中国：投資は下落幅が拡大し、消費も力強さを欠き低迷。生産は輸出堅調で底堅く推移

― 先行きは、信用スプレッド拡大など金融環境のタイト化を通じたAI投資の選別・減速が、アジアの輸出拡大に波及するリ

### Page 14

日本：7～9月期実質GDPは横ばい圏の推移。消費低調の一方、設備投資が堅調に回復

― 個人消費が2四半期連続でマイナスとなったほか、輸出が一時的要因もあり小幅減となり、成長率を下押し。一方、設備
投資はAI関連需要の堅調さを背景にプラス転換し、成長率を押し上げ
―4～6月期（前期比＋0.4％）から減速し、経済の緩やかな拡大傾向が一服
 今後、2026年度後半の実質GDPは、均してみれば小幅なプラス成長と予想
― 個人消費が精彩を欠く一方で、設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が継続。2026年度全体では＋1％弱の成長率に

Document 059: 2026年10月投資月報

### Page 1

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
利率上行引發波動，惟無損 AI 供應鏈擴張動能
2026 年 10 月
9 月全球股市普遍回落，主因為油價急升、全球長端殖利率攀高及主要央行轉鷹。僅有日本股市
受 AI、半導體需求與企業信心支撐，抵銷 BOJ 升息壓力，成為主要市場中唯一正報酬。標普 500 9
月小跌 0.3%，雖 AI 與大型科技股獲利韌性仍在，但 Fed 於 9 月升息並暗示年底前再度升息，加上
油價與美債殖利率走高，壓抑估值並引發科技股震盪。印度股市下跌 6.9%最弱，主要受油價飆升、
全球利率上行及外資單月淨流出約 27 億美元拖累。中國股市下跌 4.7%，因受內需修復偏弱、科技

股評價承壓所致；歐洲股市也因油價單月大漲近 14%與全球殖利率攀升影響，9 月下跌 4.6%。
9 月主要債市全面下跌，核心壓力來自全球殖利率急升。美歐日央行同步升息、Brent 原油單月
上漲約 14%、美國經濟數據偏強與公債供給疑慮，使美國 10 年期殖利率單月上升約 53bp，創 2022
年以來最大升幅。主要券種中，全球投資級公司債下跌 2.93%表現最弱，新興美元債亦下跌 2.91%，
因 9 月全球公債殖利率快速攀升，此兩類債券對利率變化均較敏感；其中投資級公司債相較高收益債
存續期較長、票息緩衝較低，因此在利率重新定價期間承受較大價格壓力。亞洲高收益債下挫 1.20%
相對抗跌，因高票息與較短存續期提供緩衝；新興當地債跌 1.74%，亦優於多數美元債種。
在股市方面，AI 基礎建設需求持續擴張，帶動 HBM、伺服器 DRAM、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供
應鏈維持成長動能，市場表現由全面上漲逐步轉向結構性分化。若 AI 伺服器需求持續成長且 CSP 資
本支出維持高檔，具供給優勢、定價能力及較高訂單能見度之企業，其營運表現有機會獲得支撐。整

體而言，科技產業與台灣及日本股市獲利表現仍具關注度，惟後續仍須留意全球景氣、產業供需變化、
政策監管及市場評價調整等風險因素。
就債市而言，雖然貨幣政策風險下降有助於穩定長期通膨預期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶
來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率
環境下，中天期債券於票息收益與存續期間風險之間相對較為均衡。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級
公司債指數利差約 80 個基點，雖然利差偏窄，但高基準利率環境仍提供相對較高的收益水準。因此，
在目前市場環境下，中天期美國公債及投資級債仍為值得持續觀察的債券資產類別，惟後續表現仍須
留意利率走勢、信用利差變化及經濟成長動能等因素。

美國股市：通膨與升息預期上升推動美債利率上漲，美股呈高檔區間震盪
9 月因美伊關係緊張升溫，推升油價並加劇市場對通膨回升的擔憂；相較於 6 月點陣圖，9 月FOMC
官員全面上修 2026 至 2027 年的政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示 2026 年底前會再升息一次，加上美
國經濟數據表現優於預期，使美國公債殖利率走升，進而壓抑美股表現。在此背景下，9 月標普 500
指數呈現高檔區間震盪格局。產業表現方面，資訊科技與通訊服務類股相對強勢，持續扮演指數上漲
的主要動能；相較之下，醫療保健、必需消費及部分工業類股表現較為疲弱。

9 月美國聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 的表決結果，將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調 25 個基點至
3.75%到 4.00%。9 月最新經濟預測，美國聯準會官員上修 2026 年核心 PCE 至 3.4%，2027 年維持
在 2.5%，將 2026-2027 年經濟成長上調 0.1%至 2.3%與 2.4%，顯示聯準會認為美國經濟與通膨仍
具韌性。
觀察美國近期的經濟數據可以發現企業投資動能依然強健，元大投顧表示，美國 8 月扣除飛機與
國防的核心資本財訂單由前期的月增 0.6%加速至 1.6%，其中初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均呈現
增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成長形
成穩健貢獻。
自 6 月底以來，市場持續上修標普 500 指數企業獲利預估，其中以科技與能源類股貢獻最為明顯，
反映企業營運表現優於市場預期。AI 投資循環持續推進、企業數位轉型需求增加，以及美國內需維
持穩健，共同支撐企業獲利成長動能，並有助於吸收高利率環境帶來的壓力。與此同時，標普 500

指數前瞻本益比已由先前高點回落，評價壓力較先前明顯緩解。隨著企業獲利持續改善，美股行情正
由過去仰賴資金與情緒推升評價，逐步轉向由基本面支撐股價，後續表現將更取決於企業獲利是否持
續優於預期。
投資策略方面，投資焦點已逐漸由評價擴張轉向企業獲利成長與基本面表現。短期市場仍須留意
政策面不確定性，美中貿易談判進展將牽動全球供應鏈與科技產業展望，若關稅或科技限制措施再度
升溫，可能對科技股評價造成壓力；此外，美國期中選舉逐漸接近，企業稅率、科技監管及產業政策
等議題亦可能影響市場風險偏好。整體而言，企業獲利改善仍是支撐美股最重要的力量，若 AI 投資
循環與獲利成長趨勢延續，美股中長期表現仍可望由基本面驅動。

歐洲股市：儘管企業獲利上修，仍需持續關注地緣政治發展
9 月泛歐 600 指數下跌約 2%，產業表現方面，製藥、生技、銀行及部分科技類股表現相對強勢，
顯示資金偏好具獲利能見度與防禦特性的族群；相較之下，礦業、消費及部分金融服務類股表現落後，
反映市場對歐洲經濟成長及企業獲利前景仍持審慎態度。整體而言，受到高油價以及高利率環境，投
資氣氛轉趨保守，市場資金持續聚焦於基本面較穩健的產業。
歐洲央行 9 月政策利率由 2.25% 升息 1 碼至 2.50 %，符合市場預期。歐洲央行最新經濟預
測中上修中期通膨展望，2027 年消費者物價指數年增率由 2.3% 升至 2.5% ，2028 年則由 2.0%

上調至 2.1% ；核心消費者物價指數方面，2027 年和 2028 年預測亦各上修 0.1 % 至 2.6% 及
2.3% ，能源成本上升對食品及其他價格的傳導，使得通膨回落速度較原先預期緩慢。ECB 總裁拉加
德表示能源價格上漲逐步傳導至核心及食品通膨，預期整體通膨在 2027 年上半年維持在高檔。
根據花旗歐洲獲利修正指數(Earnings Revision Index)，歐洲企業獲利預估已連續上修並到近五
年高點，反映企業基本面持續改善。受惠於歐元區景氣穩步復甦、製造業景氣回升，以及企業資本支
出逐步回暖，工業、金融及景氣循環類股獲利展望同步改善。隨著市場對企業未來盈餘預期提高，有
助支撐歐股評價面與資金流入，並成為推動泛歐 600 指數表現的重要動能。
然而，自 3 月美伊衝突爆發以來，市場對能源供給中斷的擔憂持續升溫，帶動油價走高，進而增
加仰賴進口能源的歐洲企業成本壓力，並壓抑歐股相對表現。進入 9 月後，俄羅斯與北約緊張關係再
度升溫，歐洲安全局勢不確定性提高，進一步加劇市場對能源供應與地緣政治風險的擔憂。若相關衝

突持續延燒，不僅可能推升能源價格與通膨壓力，也可能影響企業投資意願及市場風險偏好，進而對
歐洲企業獲利與股市表現形成壓力。
展望 10 月，歐股仍將受到高油價、地緣政治風險及歐洲央行維持偏鷹立場等因素影響，市場波
動性可能維持偏高。近期能源價格攀升推升通膨預期，使 ECB 傾向維持較高利率水準，不僅提高企
業融資成本，也限制股市評價進一步擴張的空間。此外，中東局勢持續緊張，以及俄羅斯與北約間的
緊張關係再度升高，均可能影響投資人風險偏好，增添歐洲金融市場不確定性。雖然歐元區經濟活動
仍維持溫和擴張，企業獲利預估亦持續改善，但在高利率與地緣政治風險尚未明顯緩解之前，市場情
緒仍偏向審慎，後續仍需持續關注能源價格、通膨走勢及地緣政治發展，作為判斷歐股能否重啟上漲
動能的重要觀察指標。

日本股市：升息落地，景氣展望佳，第四季半導體與商社企業獲利動能有望延續
9 月日本股市受升息預期及長天期公債殖利率走高影響，部分高評價股票出現震盪，但這波調整
較偏向政策轉折下的評價重估，而非企業基本面全面轉弱。日本銀行於 9 月 18 日決議將短期政策利
率目標提高至約 1.25%，使市場反覆討論的升息方向逐漸明朗。隨著政策落地，投資焦點有望由利率
變動轉回企業營運與獲利。從正面角度看，利率正常化反映日本經濟具備一定韌性，股價整理也有助
於消化先前累積的評價壓力。若後續企業財測保持穩健，9 月的震盪可望成為第四季行情重新蓄勢的

過程。
日本股市的中期支撐，來自薪資成長、內需改善與企業投資的相互帶動。2026 年春鬥最終統計
顯示，5,368 個工會的平均調薪幅度達 5.01%，連續第三年維持在 5%以上；可明確辨識的基本薪資
調升幅度亦達 3.50%。薪資持續增加，有助於提升家庭消費能力，為零售與服務等內需產業提供較穩
定的需求基礎。若企業能透過產品競爭力、定價能力及經營效率吸收成本，消費回溫便有機會轉化為
獲利成長。同時，全球 AI 相關投資也為日本半導體設備、載板及電子零組件供應鏈創造機會，使內
需復甦與科技需求形成雙重支撐。
展望 10 月，隨著升息議題逐步由預期轉為已知條件，市場可望更加重視第四季訂單、企業財測
及實際獲利。科技產業方面，若全球 AI 相關資本支出持續擴大，日本具技術優勢的設備與零組件企
業，有機會將需求轉化為接單及出貨成長；商社與資源相關企業，則可觀察能源、原物料業務及投資
收益對現金流的貢獻。內需產業亦值得留意薪資成長能否帶動消費，以及企業能否維持利潤率。整體
而言，10 月的投資機會不必侷限於單一題材，市場有望逐步從政策消息主導，轉向由產業需求與企

業業績支撐的行情。
投資策略方面，半導體供應鏈可優先挑選訂單能見度高、具技術門檻且獲利成長明確的企業；商
社則著重現金流、資本配置與股東回饋，內需股則觀察消費回溫能否反映在營收及利潤。若股價因利
率或匯率波動而短暫回落，但企業基本面與財測未明顯改變，可考慮分批布局，避免追逐短線漲幅。
後續仍須追蹤長天期利率、日圓及企業財測變化，但投資主軸可更積極地放在具備實質成長能力的公
司。日本央行升息落地後，企業獲利能否接棒，將是日本股市延續表現的關鍵。

台灣股市：訂單持續暢旺突破千億，AI 需求持續向供應鏈擴散，廠商展望樂觀
9 月觀察台股基本面，最明確的支撐來自 8 月外銷訂單創下單月新高，以及企業對 9 月接單維持
擴張預期。經濟部公布，8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，月增 5.1%、年增 71.4%；其中資訊通信
與電子產品合計 799.6 億美元，占整體 77.7%，顯示 AI 伺服器、半導體與雲端建設仍是主要成長來
源。受查廠商對 9 月接單的預期，以訂單金額計算的動向指數為 52.1，高於代表持平的 50，意味整
體接單有望較 8 月增加。雖然 9 月實際結果仍待公布，但已發布的數據與企業展望，為台股進入第

四季提供了正面的基本面依據。
更值得留意的是，AI 需求正從核心晶片延伸至更廣的供應鏈。雲端業者擴充運算能力，除了帶動
伺服器與半導體接單，也使自研 ASIC、IC 設計及相關電子零組件成為後續觀察重點；半導體投資與
設備擴產，則為自動化設備、銅箔及高階材料創造機會。8 月機械與基本金屬外銷訂單分別年增 28.3%
及 26.8%，顯示科技投資的效益已反映在部分非電子貨品。對台股而言，這有助於投資主線從少數大
型權值股，延伸至具技術優勢與訂單能見度的中上游企業。不過，面板需求仍偏弱，產業表現並非全
面同步，選股仍須辨別企業實際受惠程度。
展望 10 月，市場焦點可望轉向第三季財報、第四季營運展望，以及 9 月外銷訂單能否驗證接單
續強。就廠商對 9 月的預期而言，資訊通信與電子產品的訂單金額動向指數分別為 52.4 及 51.5，顯
示科技需求仍具支撐；基本金屬與化學品則達 59.0 及 61.0，呈現部分材料領域接單改善的期待。若
AI 伺服器出貨、自研晶片開發及半導體資本支出持續推進，相關台灣供應鏈的營收與獲利有望受惠。

另一方面，機械與光學器材指數分別為 46.3 及 49.5，提醒市場不能將整體訂單成長視為所有產業同
步復甦，仍須以後續實績檢驗。
投資策略方面，10 月以「掌握 AI 主線、尋找擴散機會、重視獲利驗證」作為配置方向。核心部
位可留意伺服器、半導體及 IC 設計等需求較明確的企業，並觀察 ASIC 相關業務能否由開發逐步轉
化為穩定出貨；其他機會則可從設備、銅箔與高階材料中，篩選確實取得訂單的公司。選股不只看產
業接單增幅，更應比較個別企業的營收成長、毛利率與獲利展望，避免在評價已高時追逐短期題材。
整體而言，台股仍有基本面支撐，後續行情能否擴大，關鍵在於 AI 需求能否持續轉化為更多企業的
實際獲利。

中國股市：AI 產業規模擴張難掩獲利困境，產業進入變現考驗
9 月中國 AI 產業的討論，從模型能力與使用量，逐漸轉向更根本的問題：在高階晶片取得受限、
業者競爭激烈的環境下，龐大的投入能否形成可持續的回報。9 月中美高層會談後，雙方宣布建立
AI 相關對話機制，但公開成果未顯示高階 AI 晶片出口限制已放寬。政策溝通有所進展，並未立即改
變企業取得先進算力的條件。與此同時，低價及開放權重策略持續降低 AI 使用門檻，帶動市場擴張，
也使企業更難單靠模型服務取得理想利潤。對投資人而言，9 月最重要的訊號不是單一產品的表現，

而是中國 AI 已進入需要同時回答算力效率、商業變現與資本回收的階段。
從整體結構來看，中國具備電力、製造基礎及廣泛應用場景，有利於 AI 以較低成本進入企業營
運與日常服務；但部署成本較低，不代表產業獲利便能同步提高。當業者以低價爭取用戶，使用量可
以迅速累積，收入卻可能跟不上基礎設施、研發及營運支出。以特定平台的觀察為例，DeepSeek 曾
取得可觀的代幣使用量，但相應支出占比明顯較低；這不能代表中國 AI 產業的整體財務表現，卻凸
顯「有人使用」與「願意付費」之間仍有距離。未來產業能否改善獲利，取決於企業是否找到具持續
需求的應用、建立合理收費方式，並避免同質化服務陷入反覆降價。
展望 10 月，中國 AI 的成長空間仍在，但市場衡量進展的標準應由使用規模，逐步轉向投入產出。
若 AI 更深入企業工作流程，相關需求有望延續；然而，需求增加後，業者仍須證明服務品質、運算
效率與客戶付費能同步改善。晶片限制使算力使用效率更加重要，價格競爭則使收入品質成為另一項
考驗。Vercel 資料顯示，8 月開放權重模型在其平台占代幣使用量 56%，支出占比卻為 14%，說明

高使用量未必對應高支出；10 月宜觀察企業是否開始披露更清楚的付費需求與成本改善，而非只比
較流量或模型排名。
投資策略方面，對中國 AI 宜維持關注成長、審慎評估獲利的態度，避免把產業滲透率提高，直
接等同於所有企業的投資價值提升。評估重點可放在三個整體問題：第一，持續增加的算力與研發投
入，能否換來足夠的收入；第二，低價競爭下，企業能否留住付費客戶並維持合理利潤；第三，AI
應用能否從試用走向長期採購。具備穩定需求、成本控制能力及清楚收費模式的企業，較有機會在產
業整合中建立優勢。整體而言，中國 AI 並非缺少使用場景，而是仍須證明大規模使用可以轉化為可
持續的獲利，這將是 10 月觀察產業與相關市場表現的核心。

科技類股：AI 應用加速落地，光通訊與 Agent 入口迎來成長機會
AI 產業的成長主軸正由模型訓練延伸至推論與實際應用，讓軟體服務與硬體建設形成相互帶動的
循環。當 AI 不再只回答問題，而能協助搜尋資訊、安排活動及完成跨平台任務，使用頻率與運算需
求有望提高。Meta 於 9 月推出個人 AI 助理 Muse，透過應用程式及 WhatsApp 接觸既有用戶，積
極爭取 AI 服務入口。對產業而言，Agent 普及不僅意味新的服務形式，也可能擴大雲端運算與資料
傳輸需求，使應用端的創新成為基礎設施升級的推力。投資機會可從使用者入口延伸至相關供應鏈。

在硬體端，AI 推論與多步驟任務若持續增加，資料中心內部的資料交換將更頻繁，高速且節能的
連接能力因而更具價值。光通訊除支援資料中心之間的傳輸，也逐步朝機櫃及晶片附近的互連場景發
展，拓展供應商的產品空間。Lumentum 指出，AI 運算對速度與頻寬的要求提升，帶動光連結需求，
並已在營收展望中反映相關成長。高速光模組與新型互連方案也持續推進。雖然部分新技術仍須經過
客戶導入與量產驗證，但既有產品需求與後續技術升級有望接續，讓光通訊成為 AI 基礎設施中值得
持續關注的環節。
在應用端，Muse 代表 AI 從被動回覆走向主動協助執行任務的嘗試。若 Agent 能替使用者完成
搜尋、比較、預訂與付款等流程，便利性提升有望促進使用黏著度，並為支付、交易與服務串接平台
創造新的合作機會。跨平台操作可能由單一入口整合，促使各產業重新思考客戶服務。「Muse 對各
產業的影響」圖可呈現商業模式轉變的可能方向。隨著更多服務接入 Agent 生態系，使用情境有望
持續擴展；後續若任務完成率、使用留存及付費轉換逐步改善，應用端便有機會形成更明確的收入來

源，並進一步支持運算與傳輸投資。
投資策略方面，建議以「應用入口與基礎設施並重」掌握 AI 從訓練走向推論的成長機會。硬體
方面，可優先關注已切入主要客戶供應鏈、具備高速光模組與新型互連產品布局，且訂單能見度較佳
的業者。應用方面，則可追蹤具備用戶基礎、服務整合能力與清楚收費模式的平台，觀察 Agent 能
否從新產品熱度轉化為持續使用與付費需求。配置上可先以業績較明確的基礎設施業者為核心，再逐
步增加應用平台的布局，兼顧當前需求與後續成長空間。整體而言，AI 應用愈深入日常生活與企業
流程，入口平台與光通訊供應鏈愈有機會相互受惠；選股關鍵仍是找出能將需求成長轉化為實際業績
的企業。

拉美市場：10 月巴西總統大選將登場，政治不確定性影響市場表現
9 月拉美市場維持震盪整理。一方面，拉美各國通膨數據普遍低於市場預期，有利市場降息預期；
另一方面，巴西總統大選選情持續拉鋸，政治不確定性升溫則讓投資人保持觀望。展望 10 月，隨巴
西大選投票日逼近，政治因素仍可能使拉美市場短線波動加劇。
受到美伊衝突帶動油價上漲影響，市場原本擔心燃料與運輸成本增加，會讓拉美物價再度大幅上
漲，進而迫使拉美央行升息。然而，高盛分析指出，拉美市場目前的通膨率仍受到控制，當地經濟承
受能源漲價的能力，比市場原先擔心的情況更好。其中一個重要原因是，新興市場政府透過燃油補貼、

稅制安排與零售價格管制，讓消費者實際感受到的價格漲幅相對有限。此外，上半年全球農產品供給
較為充裕，使食品價格漲幅偏低，也抵銷了部分能源漲價帶來的影響。由於拉美市場實際通膨壓力小
於原先市場預期，央行進一步大幅升息的必要性也隨之降低，高盛因此認為，市場目前對多數拉美經
濟體未來一至兩年的升息預期過於悲觀，並預期拉美各國央行在 2027 年較可能重新轉向降息。
不過，拉美多國經濟出現放緩跡象，亦可能壓抑股市上漲空間。智利央行於 9 月會議維持政策利
率 4.50%不變，並將 2026 年經濟成長預測下修至 0.25%至 0.75%，低於 6 月預估的 1.0%至 1.75%。
智利央行指出，第二季內需成長放緩，勞動市場及信心指標同步轉弱，加上燃料價格上漲侵蝕企業與
家庭所得，均對經濟活動形成壓力。巴西經濟亦出現放緩跡象，巴西央行於 9 月 16 日一致決議調降
Selic 政策利率 25 個基點至 13.75%，並指出景氣循環產業動能轉弱，且整體與核心通膨均已回落至
目標區間上限以下，為進一步降息提供空間。不過，巴西央行並未承諾下次會議是否將繼續降息，而

是強調將視未來經濟與通膨數據逐次評估。
另一方面，巴西總統大選進入白熱化階段則是當前影響市場最主要的因子。市場民調顯示，相較
7 月時現任總統魯拉在民調中普遍領先競爭者波索納洛約 5 至 8 個百分點，在 9 月的民調雙方差距已
縮小至 2 個百分點以內，選情不確定性仍高。若選舉最終由魯拉勝選，其支持財政擴張的政策，將可
能使市場對於巴西財政赤字問題的擔憂加劇，進而使巴西股市面臨較大壓力；而若波索納洛勝選，市
場普遍預期其能提出改善投資人信心的財政政策，為巴西央行創造更多降息空間，有利後續巴西股市
上行。
整體而言，儘管市場對拉美各國當前升息預期可能過於悲觀，但目前拉美多國經濟成長同步放
緩，加上巴西總統大選選情膠著，選後財政政策方向不確定性高，在考量大選及政策方向不確定性的
狀況下，短線拉美市場波動恐將加劇。

能源類股：全球石油庫存持續下降，但中東石油出口已出現回升
9 月西德州原油期貨價格受美伊衝突升溫影響，一度於月中重新站上每桶 100 美元，其後隨衝突
趨緩，油價漲幅逐步收斂，月底約回落至 90 美元附近，能源類股表現亦大致跟隨油價走勢。展望 10
月，近期中東石油供應恢復速度不如預期，加上庫存持續下降，預期油價下檔仍具支撐。然而，市場
數據顯示，高油價及供應受阻已同步抑制石油需求，且中東各國原油出口持續回升，後續供應緊張情
況有望逐步改善。因此，雖然中東地緣政治風險仍將對油價形成支撐，但隨美伊重啟外交接觸，油價
進一步大幅上漲的空間預期相對有限。

受到美伊談判僵持影響，國際能源署（IEA）與美國能源資訊署（EIA）在 9 月月報均將中東石油
供應恢復的預期時間延後。IEA 將中東石油供應全面恢復的預期時間延至 2027 年，並將 2026 年全
球石油供應減幅，由上月預估的每日減少 430 萬桶擴大至 570 萬桶；EIA 則將中東石油供給恢復至
衝突前水準的時間，由 2027 年初延至第 2 季。
此外，受到中東供應受阻與油價偏高影響，IEA 亦將 2026 年全球石油需求預測下修每日 94 萬桶，
預估全年需求將較去年減少每日 250 萬桶，EIA 則預估全年石油需求將減少約每日 170 萬桶，石油
輸出國組織（OPEC）也將今年需求由上月預估的每日增加 60 萬桶下修至 40 萬桶。
受此影響，全球石油庫存持續下降。IEA 估計，8 月全球可觀察石油庫存減少 9,500 萬桶，自 2
月底以來已累計下降 5.07 億桶，顯示市場仍持續動用庫存填補缺口。EIA 也預期全球石油庫存將持
續下降至年底，因此將 2026 年下半年布蘭特原油均價預測，由上月約每桶 82 美元上調至 90 美元；

此外，隨著 2027 年中東出口與產量逐步恢復，IEA 預估明年全年布蘭特原油均價將回落至 74 美元。
另一方面，中東原油出口則已經出現回升跡象。路透社 9 月 28 日引述 Kpler 初步資料指出，沙
烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋 9 月原油出口均出現上升，帶動中東主要產油國當月原油日均出口量回升至每日
1,280 萬桶，創下 2 月戰爭爆發以來最高水準。其中，沙烏地阿拉伯 9 月原油日均出口預估將達每日
540 萬桶，較 8 月的 244.6 萬桶增加逾一倍。由於沙國東西向輸油管線先前因攻擊受損，沙烏地計
畫將部分原先經紅海延布港出口的原油，轉往波斯灣沿岸港口裝運，帶動拉斯坦努拉港 9 月日均出貨
量預估由 8 月的每日 92.9 萬桶增至 360 萬桶。
整體而言，中東供應恢復時程延後，加上全球石油庫存持續下降，短期仍有利支撐油價。然而，
石油需求預估同步下修，且沙烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋原油出口均已逐步回升，後續石油供應緊張程度可
望有所緩解。整體而言，儘管油價仍受中東地緣風險支撐，但隨美國期中選舉接近，近期美伊亦重啟

外交接觸，市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂可望逐步降溫，油價進一步大幅上漲空間相對有限。

黃金類股：升息預期攀升壓抑金價表現；財富配置及央行買盤支撐中長期需求
近期黃金價格表現較為疲弱，主要受到市場升息預期持續攀升，帶動美債殖利率走高，增加持有
黃金的機會成本，對於利率較為敏感的黃金 ETF 需求因此受到壓抑。然而，高盛研究指出，聯準會
近期決議升息雖然延長了市場高利率環境時間，但並不會進一步墊高利率終點，加上全球央行持續購
金，以及投資人從財富配置角度增加黃金配置，均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。
高盛報告指出，聯準會於 9 月升息一碼，增加持有黃金的機會成本，使對利率敏感的黃金 ETF 需
求下滑，高盛因此將 2026 年底金價預估由每盎司 4,900 美元下修至 4,650 美元。然而，高盛也預估，

聯準會將於 2027 年 9 月至 2028 年 3 月期間降息三碼，使政策利率最終回到 3.25%至 3.5%區間，
故儘管聯準會 9 月升息延長了高利率環境，但實際並未墊高利率終點，因此來自黃金 ETF 的賣壓僅
會延後金價上漲時程，但並未改變高盛對黃金的偏多看法。
另一方面，央行買盤仍是金價支撐核心。高盛 9 月報告預估，全球央行近三個月經季節調整後的
購金速度已上升至每月 91 噸，遠高於 2022 年前每月 17 噸的平均水準。高盛指出，這股需求主要
來自分散外匯儲備的考量，在 2022 年俄羅斯央行資產遭美國凍結後，各國央行更加重視儲備資產遭
制裁或凍結的風險，相較於對利率敏感的 ETF 資金，央行基於儲備分散而進行的購金，不易因短期
利率變動而轉向，因此即使聯準會升息，央行買盤仍有利支撐金價表現。
除了央行買盤外，花旗也從全球財富配置的角度看好黃金。花旗指出，全球每年新增黃金礦產供
應規模相當有限，若全球家庭額外將 0.1%的財富配置於黃金，就相當於約 4,459 噸黃金購買量，也

等於 1.2 年的新增礦產供應量。換言之，即使全球投資人僅小幅提高黃金配置比重，也可能創造遠超
過新增供給的需求。由於黃金供給短期內難以快速增加，市場往往需要透過金價上漲，吸引既有持有
人釋出黃金以滿足新增需求，因此資產配置需求增加可能對金價產生顯著推升影響。
不過，花旗也指出，黃金新增供給有限的特性雖有助於放大需求對金價的推升效果，但同時也可
能提高金價出現較劇烈修正的風險。花旗估計，2022 年以來的金價上漲已使既有黃金持有人的帳面
財富增加約 13 兆美元，若持有人將其中約 1 兆美元轉回股票、債券或現金，所形成的黃金賣壓便可
能超過目前全部實體黃金需求，故當持有人集中獲利了結時，金價也可能出現較劇烈的修正。
整體而言，在市場升息預期持續攀升的情況下，短期黃金 ETF 需求可能受到壓抑，金價仍可能面
臨震盪。然而，全球央行基於外匯儲備分散需求持續購金，加上投資人從財富配置角度提高黃金配置，
均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。

固定收益：聯準會升息穩定通膨預期，凸顯中天期美債與投資級債吸引力
美國 9 月聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 全票通過，將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調 25 個基點至
3.75%–4.00%。最新經濟預測同步將 2026 年核心 PCE 上修至 3.4%、GDP 成長率上修至 2.3%，且
全面上修 2026 至 2027 年政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示年底前仍有一次升息空間。9 月聯準會採取
預防性升息，主要是在避免通膨預期鬆動並維持政策公信力，確立高利率維持更久的政策基調。
美國政府於 9 月 30 日公布第二季國內生產毛額(GDP)終值，經季節調整後成長年率由 1.5%上修

至 2.2%，高於經濟學家預估的維持不變。第一季 GDP 季增年率也由 2.1%上修至 2.5%。其中，占
美國經濟活動逾 6 成的消費支出，第二季成長率由 3.4%上修至 3.8%，顯示美國消費動能強勁；元
大投顧指出，美國 8 月核心資本財訂單月增率加速至 1.6%，其中初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均
呈現增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成
長形成穩健貢獻。在消費與企業動能表現強勁下，使市場重新評估美國經濟對通膨黏性的影響。
彭博 10 月 1 日的報告指出，在 Market Pulse 調查的 173 名受訪者，超過一半的人預測美國 30
年期公債殖利率在年底將達到 6%。儘管伊朗戰爭和高借貸成本持續影響，美國經濟仍維持強勁成長，
這種韌性加劇了通膨和進一步升息的⾵險；人工智慧基礎設施建設競賽引發了企業發債的狂潮，推升
長天期債券發行量，全球企業今年以來已發行超過 4000 億美元的債券，用於技術投資，而其中大部
分債券發行於美國。另外，各國政府持續的預算赤字，美國聯邦政府債務規模首次突破 40 兆美元，

美國政府需要擴大發債量，投資人要求更高的利率來購買債券等因素，皆為推升美國長天期殖利率上
行的原因。因此，長天期債券後續表現仍須持續觀察利率走勢、債券供需變化及通膨發展。
展望 10 月，由於美國通膨仍高於聯準會 2%的目標，2026 年底有再升息一碼的可能，使得短
天期利率下行空間有限。在政策利率可能維持高檔、長天期殖利率又受到供給面壓力推升的環境下，
當前的債券配置應以「息收防禦」與「存續期間控管」為核心。隨著美國公債利率上行，彭博美國投
資級債指數平均剩餘到期期限持續下降，存續期間縮短有助降低利率變動對債券價格的影響。美國債
市投資建議重點配置中天期美國公債與投資級公司債。中天期美國公債與投資級公司債不僅能鎖定具
吸引力的收益率水準，也能兼顧存續期間風險控管，降低利率波動對投資組合淨值的衝擊，在當前環
境下有助於兼顧收益與風險控制。

2026年 11月 LPR报价保持稳定，短期内下调可能性不大 
 王青  冯琳 
2026年 11月 20日，中国人民银行授权全国银行间同业拆借中心公布新版 LPR报价：1
年期品种报 3.0%，上月为 3.0%；5年期以上品种报 3.5%，上月为 3.5%。 
解读：

11月两个期限品种的 LPR报价保持不变，符合市场预期。11月初，央行优化短端利率调
控机制，宣布自 11月 2日起每日开展固定利率的隔夜逆回购操作招标，并明确目前操作利率
是 1.25%；7天期逆回购操作调整为多重价位中标，政策利率属性淡化。这意味着，央行主要
政策利率从 7天期向隔夜“换锚”。“换锚”后，DR001 的走廊区间从 1.15%-1.65%下调至
1.0%-1.5%。但需要指出的是，这并非政策利率下调，也意味着 11月 LPR报价的定价基础没
有变化。另外，尽管近期 DR001 等主要货币市场利率、银行同业存单到期收益率、存款利率
都有不同程度的下行，但受贷款利率持续走低等影响，当前银行净息差仍然处在历史低位附
近，报价行缺乏主动下调 LPR报价加点的动力。 
近期 LPR报价保持稳定，根本原因是三季度 GDP增速回升到 4.5%，出口延续高增，以高
技术制造业为代表的新质生产力领域加速发展；10 月宏观数据有所走弱，但更多属于月度正
常波动，四季度经济增长动能仍有上行趋势，完成全年“4.5%至 5.0%”的经济增长目标难度
不大。在此背景下，近期包括全面降息在内的总量货币政策保持定力。 
展望未来，短期内 LPR报价下调的可能性不大。主要原因在于，9月 29日央行调整完善
若干结构性货币政策工具，包括 PSL 利率下调 0.25 个百分点，支农支小再贷款额度上调
5000亿，其中民企再贷款额度上调 3000亿，科技创新和技术改造再贷款额度上调 2000亿，
“报销”比例由原来的 60%上调至 100%；与此同时，财政部门也会同央行、金融监管总局，
首次在全国范围内出台了针对部分首套刚需购房贷款的贴息政策。这意味着逆周期调节已优
先选择结构性政策工具发力。我们判断，在以上结构性稳增长、稳楼市措施出台后，接下来
将进入一段政策效果观察期，短期内全面降息等总量货币政策工具跟进的可能性下降，LPR
报价的定价基础会保持稳定。 
拉长时间来看，综合考虑经济金融演化趋势，预计 2027年央行有望全面降息 10到 20个

3Q26 GM slightly lower: Off revenue of NT$120,744mn (+26% QoQ/+66% YoY),

$13,748mn (EPS NT$24.5).
Outlook still on optimistic side: We expect Accton to grow revenue sequentially
into 4Q26. Network switch customers will likely continue to pull in more for their AI
clusters amid 800G migration. Meanwhile, its AI accelerator customer should ramp
more of its new generation product and increase adoption of liquid cooling thermal
solutions. To mitigate pressure from elevated component costs, it has negotiated

with customers for them to consign some components, e.g., DRAM, from late 3Q26.
Although this might cap revenue growth upside for Accton, it should help on the
GM side. We model revenue of NT$130,083mn (+8% QoQ/+81% YoY), gross margin
of 20.0% (+2.4ppt QoQ), operating margin of 15.5% (+2.1ppt QoQ)  and EPS of NT
$28.2 (+21% QoQ/+88% YoY). Looking to 2027, we see revenue growth of NT
$526,019mn (+26% YoY) and GM of 20.0%, for EPS NT$112.9 EPS (+31% YoY).

Stay OW: We believe Accton should continue to benefit from networking and
compute demand growth amid AI deployment, reflecting its system integration
capability, customer relationships, and execution. For the longer term,  it will likely
tap into opportunities in rack level solutions, optical-based switches, etc. We lower
our PT to NT$2,250 (20x 2027 P/E) from NT$3,500 (30x 2027 P/E), mostly owing
to more conservative sentiment toward AI names.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
OW with PT of NT$2,250 (previously NT$3,500)
Price target (base case scenario value) –  NT$2,250 (-36%): Our new price target is based
on 20x our 2027 EPS estimate (30x previously). We lower our target multiple to factor in
recent market sentiment toward AI names. We see our target multiple supported by the
45% operating profit CAGR we project for 2025-28. This implies a PEG of 0.4 vs. its
average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for an operating

profit CAGR of 32%.
Bull case scenario value – NT$3,350 (-20%): We apply 30x to our base case EPS estimate
for 2027 (36x previously). This scenario reflects stronger demand for network switches in
both data center and enterprise applications as well as more orders for AI accelerator
modules.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,800 (-39%): We apply 10x to our base case EPS estimate
for 2027 (16x previously). This scenario reflects lukewarm data center and enterprise

capex and continued dominance by merchant GPUs in AI servers.
Downside risks to our positive thesis
• Weaker data center capex
• Slower network switch spec migration
• InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
• Softer custom AI accelerator growth
• Softer ODM direct business model adoptions

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Update
Lotes Co. Ltd  | Asia Pacific
3Q GM Decline Eases; 4Q
Recovery in Focus
3Q results:
• GM came in at 44.8% (-60bps q/q, -640bps y/y), 20bps above our estimate.
3Q GM continued to face margin headwinds from rising raw material costs,
which we highlighted in our recent upgrade report here. We look forward to
management's comment during its results call to see if 4Q margin will be
guided to return to growth.
• Opex was  higher than expected at NT$2.2bn, 10% above our estimate and

9% above the Street.
• So OpM wa only 22.9% (+100bps q/q, -700bps y/y), 180bps below our
estimate and 250bps below the Street.
• Net profit was NT$1,919mn (-3% q/q, -17% y/y) for EPS of NT$17.09, 12%
below our estimate and 12% below the Street.
• Lotes is holding an earnings call on November 13 at 4 PM HKT; we will
provide takeaways after the call.

Key Takeaways
3Q GM fell 60bps q/q, to 44.8%, 20bps above our estimate but 70bps below the
Street. The sequential decline continued to narrow.

EPS of NT$17.09 missed our estimate and the Street by 12%, with opex 10%
above our estimate/9% above the Street.
We believe the new server CPU socket production ramp in 4Q can support our
view of 4Q GM recovery.
The call tomorrow should help clarify that and the opex overshoot.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lotes Co. Ltd. (3533.TW)
Primary - Residual Income
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.6% (beta of
1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7% and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected growth in the server business, such as from cloud and enter-
prise in China

n Stronger-than-expected DT demand
n Sooner-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n Faster ramp-up of its EV business
Risks to Downside
n Further deceleration of server demand from US hyperscalers
n Weaker-than-expected DT demand
n Slower-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n More severe pricing erosion driven by competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

研 究 報 告                
力旺(3529.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
先進與成熟製程同步發酵，營運逐季創高至 2027 年無虞 
 投資評價與建議 
公司地位居產業頂端，全球布局完善，先進製程貢獻推升與成熟製程漲價同步
推營運向上，此外，隨著Caliptra規範推升更多客戶導入公司產品，PUF有望
成為營運主⼒，⻑期營運成⻑無虞，近期股價因系統性風險影響反而浮現投資
價值。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26先進製程與成熟製程同步推升營收與獲利同創新高：受惠先進製程單價
提升更有助營收認列，及成熟製程漲價效應持續發酵，推估營收13.2億元，
QoQ+8.7%，EPS 9.45元。 
營運逐季成⻑將由2026年延續至2027年：展望未來，1)來自先進製程貢獻持
續發酵，由於先進製程單價更高，單⼀授權⾦單價數倍於平均，營收助益將更
大，未來權利⾦可望明顯提升；2)成熟製程回溫：因AI周邊的外溢效果帶動晶
圓代工廠產能利用率提升，自2Q26開始逐步漲價，而晶圓代工將持續漲價至
2027年，⼒旺自3Q26開始同步受惠；3)PUF自2Q25起，授權⾦及權利⾦貢獻
開始放大，其中大部分來自先進製程，隨著 AI 持續蓬勃發展，伴隨全球性資
安事件層出不窮，硬體安全需求有望加速進展，無論是Caliptra開始逐步建立
相關規範或Hardware Root of Trust正從選配變成標配，PUF潛在營運驚人。
整體而言，2026年營運逐季持續成⻑並續創新高將延續至2027年。 
 投資風險 
先進製程進展不如預期。

| 營運概況 
3Q26營收連四季創新高，獲利符合預期：1)受惠先進製程貢獻提升，授權⾦續創新高，其中PUF授權⾦1.6億元，
再創新高，顯示需求持續發酵；2)受惠成熟製程漲價，權利⾦7.7億元也創新高，其中Neofuse權利⾦5.4億元、PUF
權利⾦0.14億元、MTP權利⾦0.25億元，同創新高，整體3Q26營收12.1億元，QoQ+10.4%，明顯超越10億元規
模且連四季創新高，費用4.5億元則控制有成，EPS 8.56元，優於預期。 
4Q26先進製程與成熟製程同步貢獻，營收與獲利有望同創新高：10月營收7.26億元創新高，主要來自權利⾦5.94
億元(創新高)，除了先進製程單價提升更有助營收認列，及成熟製程漲價效應持續發酵(營收認列主要以晶圓代工
廠前⼀季營收為主，成熟製程主要自2Q26開始漲價)，推估營收13.2億元，QoQ+8.7%，EPS 9.45元。 
營運逐季成⻑將由2026年延續至2027年：展望未來，主要來自市場對先進製程技術、AI 相關應用、安全解決方
案的需求持續強勁，帶動授權⾦持續成⻑趨勢，及權利⾦成⻑動能可望加速，主要來自於新增先進製程應用帶來
的更高單價、新產品應用放量、PUF權利⾦貢獻擴大，以及具較高權利⾦的MTP產品占比提升，其中1)來自先進製
程貢獻持續發酵，由於先進製程單價更高，單⼀授權⾦單價數倍於平均，營收助益將更大，目前授權⾦NRE的佔
比，已經超過50%，未來權利⾦可望明顯提升，且3nm已有客戶量產成功，並持續朝2nm開發，未來對於權利⾦
貢獻將更顯著，因此3Q26無論是授權⾦或權利⾦同為新高或次高⽔準，未來將更具成⻑潛⼒；2)成熟製程回溫：
因AI周邊的外溢效果帶動晶圓代工廠產能利用率提升，自2Q26開始逐步漲價，而晶圓代工將持續漲價至2027年，
⼒旺自3Q26開始同步受惠，此外，既有應用升級帶動權利⾦單價提升(如16nm 折疊式手機OLED DDI，PMIC從
8吋轉到55nm)，對於營收結構仍以成熟製程為主的⼒旺將是⼀大福⾳；3)PUF自2Q25起，授權⾦及權利⾦貢獻
開始放大，其中大部分來自先進製程，隨著 AI 持續蓬勃發展，目前Security 並非主要考量，而Caliptra僅開始逐
步建立相關規範，但近期全球性資安事件層出不窮，硬體安全需求有望加速進展，此外，Hardware Root of Trust 
正從選配變成標配，PUF潛在營運驚人，隨著製程進入2nm，晶片設計與製造成本持續提高，客戶對面積、功耗、
可靠度以及符合安全標準的要求也更為嚴格，NeoFuse及PUF-based security IP的競爭優勢也更加明顯。整體而
言，2026年營運逐季持續成⻑並續創新高將延續至2027年。 
營運逐季創高至2027年，中⻑期成⻑無虞：公司地位居產業頂端，全球布局完善，先進製程貢獻推升與成熟製程
漲價同步推營運向上，此外，隨著Caliptra規範推升更多客戶導入公司產品，PUF有望成為營運主⼒，⻑期營運成
⻑無虞，近期股價因系統性風險浮現投資價值，投資建議維持買進，目標價3,550元。

6415 矽力杰( Silergy Corp.)

報告日期：2026/11/16          研究員：王蕎程(Joe Wang) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要: 
 本業獲利持續改善：3Q26 營業利益季增 29.7%，營益率提升
至 13.26%；惟 2Q26 受金融資產評價利益挹注，墊高比較基
期，第三季獲利下滑主要來自業外收益回落，並非本業衰退。 
 AIDC 與車用雙引擎帶動成長：AIDC 營收年增逾 120%，新一
代 SPS 預計 2027 年開始放量；車用受惠 MCU 及高壓大電流
產品導入，帶動單車價值提升，預期 2027 年兩大業務營收均
有望成長逾 50%。 
 製程升級與供貨優勢支撐成長：Gen4 產品持續放量，預期 2027
年底營收占比達 50%，搭 配 12 吋產能布局及 Gen5 新品量產，
有利爭取大型客戶，惟封測產能吃緊及成本上升仍為毛利改善
的觀察重點。

一、3Q26 本業獲利持續改善，預估 4Q26 EPS 2.5 元 
矽力*-KY 為電源管理 IC 設計廠，3Q26 營收 72.59 億元（QoQ 
+20.9%、YoY +52.4%），主要受惠 AIDC、工業及車用需求成長，
以及消費電子客戶提前拉貨。毛利率 47.40%，QoQ +0.4 個百分點，
主要因產品組合改善；營業利益 9.63 億元（QoQ +29.7%），營業
利益率 13.26%，QoQ +0.88 個百分點，本業獲利持續改善。惟 2Q26
受金融資產評價利益 9.59 億元挹注，墊高獲利基期，3Q26 因業外
損益回落至 1.11 億元，稅後淨利降至 9.09 億元（QoQ -42.1%），
EPS 2.34 元，獲利下滑主要來自業外收益減少，並非本業衰退。
展望 4Q26，消費電子需求受第三季提前拉貨及終端市場疲弱影
響，手機、電視等產品出貨轉弱，惟資料中心需求仍維持成長，
預估營收 74.63 億元，毛利率 47.80%，稅後 EPS 2.50 元。

二、產品組合優化，AIDC 與車用為明年主要成長動能 
AIDC 為公司主要成長來源，3Q26 營收占比達 17%，YoY +120%。
隨 AI 伺服器 CPU 及 GPU 功耗提高，主板供電系統需要處理更大
電流，同時降低電力損耗及散熱負擔，帶動高效率電源管理 IC 需
求。其中，新一代 SPS（Smart Power Stage）將功率元件、驅動器
及感測功能整合於單一封裝，可改善高電流供電效率並縮小電路
板空間，目前已進入多家客戶驗證階段，預期 2027 年開始貢獻營
收。公司表示，北美客戶對相關產品的需求較為迫切，預期放量
速度將快於中國客戶；此外，伺服器主板、資料中心通訊設備及
SSD 為 AIDC 主要成長應用，三者合計可占 AI 相關營收約 70%～

80%。公司預期 2027 年 AIDC 營收成長逾 50%，至 2027 年底營收

三、車用單車價值提升，光通訊與 12 吋製程布局 
車用方面，公司持續增加單一車款的晶片供應種類，除既有電源
管理產品外，高壓、大電流電源 IC、MCU 及電池管理相關產品陸
續導入域控制器、電驅等應用，帶動單車產品價值提升。公司表
示，高階車款單車產品價值預計由 2026 年底的 200～220 美元，提
升至 2027 年的 270～300 美元；海外新客戶近期已開始小批量出
貨，明年貢獻可望增加，惟中國客戶仍為主要營收來源，預期 2027
年車用營收成長逾 50%。光通訊方面，隨 AI 資料中心傳輸速率提
升，光模組對供電效率、功耗及散熱的要求同步提高，帶動高效
率電源轉換、雷射驅動電源及整合式電源管理產品需求。公司已
布局 NPO 及 CPO 相關產品，並進入客戶設計導入階段。

製程方面，公司布局 12 吋晶圓產能，目前的產能可支應至少 20
億美元營收規模。隨 AIDC 及車用需求增加，類比 IC 供應鏈產能
趨緊，部分同業仍以 8 吋製程為主，矽力則因 12 吋產能準備較為
充足，有利爭取重視供貨穩定性的大型客戶。公司表示，Gen4 產
品持續放量，預期 2027 年底營收占比約 50%，Gen5 則預計於 2027
年量產。新製程除改善成本結構外，也能支援 AIDC 對電源轉換效
率、功率密度及產品整合度的要求，有利提升高階產品比重。

供應鏈方面，目前主要瓶頸在封裝測試，受設備供應不足及裝機
速度限制，產品生產週期由原先約 4 個月延長至 9 個月，公司須
提前備妥晶圓等待後段排產，帶動在製品增加。預期 2027 年上半
年封測產能仍難明顯改善，較大幅度的產能紓解可能落在 2027 年
下半年至 2028 年。雖然公司前段晶圓供應相對充足，但後段產能
限制仍可能影響出貨速度，且成本上升未必能完全轉嫁客戶。

四、預估 2026 / 2027 年稅後 EPS 10.87 / 14.72 元 
預估 2026 年營收 255.85 億元（YoY +36.0%），毛利率 47.58%，營
業利益 31.33 億元，稅後 EPS 10.87 元。展望 2027 年，受惠 AIDC
及車用新品放量、Gen4 良率改善與高階產品比重提升，預估營收
319.81 億元（YoY +25.0%），毛利率提升至 50.07%；營業利益 57.83
億元（YoY +84.6%）。考量業外收益回歸正常水準，預估稅後 EPS 
14.72 元（YoY +35.4%）。 
五、投資建議「買進」，目標價 600 元 
綜合考量：(1) AIDC 需求持續成長，帶動伺服器主板、SSD 及光

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一. 公司簡介 
矽力杰股份有限公司（簡稱：矽力 KY；代號：6415）主要從事類
比、數位混和 IC 之研發設計及銷售，產品應用範圍廣泛，主要
應用於 3C 產業、雲端伺服器、工業類、汽車、5G 通訊等相關
領域，包括 LED 照明、平板電腦、筆記型電腦、固態硬碟、視
頻監控、伺服器、數位機上盒、智能 音箱、智慧型手機、多媒體
電視、LED 背光模組、路由器、行動電源、汽車電子、通訊基地
台及智能電錶等，並持續增加各種終端應用，如 TV Panel、手機
快充、醫療領域等。

矽力 KY 目前產品主要是類比 IC 晶片，產品廣泛應用於消費性
電子產品、工業用產品、資訊產品、5G 網路通訊產品及汽車電子
產品。針對終端產品設計需求，該公司可提供不同的晶片，例如
平板電腦、電池充電管理晶片、直流對直流轉換晶片、過流保護
晶片、靜電防護裝置、LED 背光驅動晶片以及 PMU 等；針對 LED 
照明，該公司可提供 LED 照明驅動晶片；針對機上盒，該公司
可提供交流對直流轉換晶片，直流對直流轉換晶片，過流保護晶
片，EMI 防護裝置等；智慧電表，則提供包含能源監控管理(Energy 
measurement IC)的系統晶片 System on Chip (SOC) 以及高精度電
流檢測方案。

二. 產業趨勢概況 
市場概況 
(1)半導體市場概況與 IC 設計業 
半導體產業為工業類設備、資訊電腦、網路通訊、消費性及汽車
電子產品應用的關鍵性產業，亦是目前主導性的基礎產業之一，
隨著 3C(電腦 Computer、通訊 Communication、消費 Consumer)產
品的蓬勃發展和工業類產品智慧化和低能耗的需求，產品生命週
期縮短而更加快速，並朝多元化及豐富化發展，隨著產品推陳出
新與成長，均促進未來半導體需求及市場穩定發展。國內半導體
產業鏈發展日益成熟，有完整的上、中、下游分工體系，使得 IC 
設計公司得以專精於從事設計工作，再將 IC 的製造委託給晶圓
代工製作成晶圓半成品，經過前段測試後再交由封裝廠進行切割
及封裝，最後再經專業測試廠做後段測 試後，完成最後產品，也
造就了良好的 IC 設計產業發展環境。 IC 元件一般可分為數位
邏輯 IC 及類比 IC 兩大類。數位 IC 利用 0、1 非連續性方式
傳遞訊號，多用於處理數位資訊如計算、控制、儲存等，強調尺
寸、速度與功耗(Process Driven)。類比 IC 則負責處理來自自然界
的連續性類比訊號，如光、 熱、電、速度、壓力等物理量，並作
為橋接上述各類物理訊息與數位電子系統的媒介，需同時藉由製
程、電路設計和半導體元件物理的相互配合，在晶片效能及成本 
上尋求最適化，由於其決定了數位產品所能呈現的最終呈現品

質，因此更為注重元件的特性(Design Driven)，琢磨於可靠度、穩
定度、功率消耗、能源轉換效率、電壓電流控制能力等

(2)電源管理 IC 
隨著環保與節能議題發酵，節能科技為目前重要發展課題之一，
而類比 IC 中 的電源管理 IC 正扮演著不可或缺的關鍵性角
色，電源管理 IC 主要作為在不同的系統運作狀態下，隨時控制
並保持系統內適當電流與電壓正常供給。而電源供應器的好壞，
將會直接影響電子產品的使用壽命。近年來電源供應器均朝向輕
薄短小、省電、耐用的方向發展。而要產品達到這些目標的重要
關鍵，乃在於如何把電源供應器做到高效率(高效能)、高穩壓率和
低雜訊干擾，這其中所有參與管制功能的 IC 即被稱為「電源控
制、管理類比 IC」，每年市場均保持穩定成長，消費電子、5G 網
路通信、移動互聯領域、雲端伺服器、工業應用都是目前最主要
市場，未來並朝向人工智慧及電動車及車連網方向發展。電源管
理單元(PMU)是一種高度集成的電源管理方案，將傳統分立的若干
類電源管理器件整合在單個的封裝之內，可實現更高的電源轉換
效率、更低功耗，以及更少的元件數。PMU 在有限的體積內集成
了盡可能多的電源通道，根據各個子單元的需求、提供高效率的
供電，或提供低雜訊的乾淨電源，又或者是系統必須的上電時序
控制，成為為系統供電的首選。過去十年智慧型手持式裝置在市
場快速發展，總體來看，終端市場整體還將保持快速成長，在此
大背景下，PMU 的市場需求有望實現較之前市場預期更高的發展
態勢。隨著智慧型手持式產品的大螢幕化、輕薄化，電池的體積
也將會越來越小，在相應的電池容量不變的情況下，要求整機的
續航能力要更強，除了不斷降低晶片功耗改善以外，對電源的智
慧化管理也變得不可或缺，因此未來 PMU 市場還會有不錯發展。

未來展望 
電源管理 IC 是類比 IC 最基礎和重要的產品，近年來電源管理
一直是半導體領域的熱門市場之一，且電源管理 IC 是所有電子
產品必備的關鍵零組件，屬於高功率類比電路技術，主要用途為
提供電子產品穩定且適當電壓的電源，將直接影響電子產品的使
用壽命。隨著全球 3C 產品的功能不斷增加，汽車自動駕駛和工
業自動 化需求，終端應用漸漸走向低耗電、輕薄短小與多功能整
合以及對產品壽命與可靠度要求更高的趨勢，為達到此目的，電
源管理 IC 必須做到使電源系統效率提升、節能及縮小 IC 元件
封裝以減少所佔用之電路板面積等重點。另隨著 CPU 不斷向上 
躍進，對電源的穩定性及電壓的精準度也就更為要求。此外，高
整合度不但能減少零件數量，達到降低系統耗電以及提升系統可
靠度及品質等效益，並可提高生產良率，且降低生產工時，進而

降低成本。而隨著環保與節能的議題發酵，節能科技為目前重要
發展課題之一，電源管理 IC 扮演不可或缺的關鍵角色。除低耗
電、極低的靜態消耗電流等需求外，低耗電使用無鉛封裝製程亦
是全球各國對環保、低耗能等綠色概念的要求重點，而電源管理 
IC 同時也得考量未來環保法規帶來的限制，故符合環保及綠色能
源將成為電源管理 IC 設計的重要方向。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一、預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 2.50 元 
矽力*-KY 3Q26 營收 72.59 億元（QoQ +20.9%、YoY +52.4%），受
惠 AIDC、工業及車用需求成長，毛利率 47.40%，營業利益 9.63
億元（QoQ +29.7%）；惟 2Q26 金融資產評價利益墊高比較基期，
3Q26 業外收益回落，稅後淨利 9.09 億元（QoQ -42.1%），EPS 2.34
元。展望 4Q26，消費電子需求轉弱，惟資料中心需求維持成長，
預估營收 74.63 億元（QoQ +2.8%），毛利率 47.80%，稅後淨利 9.76
億元，EPS 2.50 元。

二、預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 10.87 元 
預估 2026 年營收 255.85 億元（YoY +36.0%），受惠 AIDC 及車用
需求增加，毛利率 47.58%，營業毛利 121.74 億元；營業利益 31.33
億元，營業利益率 12.25%，業外損益 15.70 億元，稅後淨利 42.24
億元，稅後 EPS 10.87 元。

三、預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 14.72 元 
展望 2027 年，受惠 AIDC 新一代 SPS 產品導入、車用單車價值提
升，以及 Gen4 製程良率改善與高階產品比重增加，預估全年營收
319.81 億元（YoY +25.0%），營業毛利 160.12 億元，毛利率 50.07%；
營業利益 57.83 億元（YoY +84.6%），營業利益率 18.08%，業外
損益 4.51 億元，稅後淨利 57.34 億元（YoY +35.7%），稅後 EPS 14.72
元。

2026.10.30   
현대차 (005380)

26.3Q Review: 이제 올라가야 할 때 Company Brief

본업 Review: 26.3Q 매출액 45.1조원, 영업이익 2.6조원  
현대차는 26.3Q 매출액 45.1원 (-3.4% YoY, -8.3% QoQ), 영업이익 2.6조원 (+5.5% 
YoY, -6.1% QoQ, +5.9% OPM) 등의 실적을 시현했다 [iM증권 추정치 매출액: 
45.3조원, 영업이익: 2.4조원]. 매출액은 시장 컨센서스에 부합했고, 영업이익의 경우 
시장 컨센서스였던 2.4조원을 소폭 상회했다. 주요 구성 요소 별 내용은 아래와 같다.  
① P: 26.3Q HEV 판매 대수는 16.6만대를 기록하며 전사 판매 대비 비중 
18.2% 달성 (+18.8% YoY, -11.1% QoQ). 미국의 경우 국내 공장 생산 
감소에 따른 수출 부진으로 도매 판매는 소폭 감소했으나, 판매 비중은 
25.2%로 전반적인 믹스는 직전 분기와 유사. 26.3Q 평균환율이 직전 분기 
대비 크게 하락했으나, 전년 동기 기저 탓에 오히려 + 환율 효과 관찰됨. 
② Q: 26.3Q 도매 판매 대수는 91.8만대로 전년 동기 대비 11.5% 감소 (이하 
YoY 기준, 미국: -10.0%, 유럽: -18.1% 국내: -27.2% 인도: +18.3%). 
26.2Q 안전 공업 화재에 이어 26.3Q에 발생한 파업의 영향으로 생산 
대수는 감소. 국내 공장에서의 생산 차질은 내수 판매에 더해 수출 감소, 즉 
전체적인 판매 축소로 연결됨. 26.4Q부터 생산 관련 노이즈는 발생하지 
않을 전망. 이에 더해 아반떼, 투싼 등의 신차 판매 효과를 통한 반등 가능.  
③ C: 26.3Q 관세 비용은 직전 분기와 유사한 수준으로 발생한 것으로 파악 
(25.2Q 0.8조원, 25.3Q 1.8조원, 25.4Q 1.5조원, 26.1Q 0.8조원, 26.2Q 
0.9조원). 이란 사태에 따른 주요 원자재 가격 상승 압력은 계속 존재하나, 
내부 원가 절감 정책으로 약 5,000억원의 원가 상승분을 상쇄. 현대차는 
26.4Q 원가 절감 정책의 효과가 더 크게 나타날 것이라고 언급. 26.3Q 
기말 환율이 전년 동기 대비 급락함에 따라 판매보층충당부채전입액 또한 
감소 (26.3Q 매출원가, 판매관리비 각 전년 동기 대비 1.7%, 19.2% 감소). 
금융 부문의 경우 26.3Q 매출액 9.1조원 (+27.8% YoY), 영업이익 4,710억원 (-
18.2% YoY, -37.9%) 등의 실적을 기록했다. 완성차 판매가 전반적으로 
위축되었음에도 외형 성장을 이어갔으나, 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 등 전반적인 
조달 관련 비용이 증가했다. 일부 EV를 중심으로 중고차 가격이 소폭 하락한 점 또한 
HCA 등 주요 부문 이익 감소의 배경이다. 26.3Q 현대차 순이익은 1,932억원을 
기록했는데, 이는 영업외손익이 전년 동기 789억원에서 229억원으로 감소했기 
때문이다. 현대차는 주요 환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실 증가의 영향이라고 밝혔다. 
1) 생산 단에서의 노이즈가 연속해 발생하며 내수 판매 및 수출 감소가 불가피했으나, 
2) 원자재 가격 상승 영향을 내부 원가 절감 정책을 통해 상쇄했고, 3) 주요 
평균환율이 하락했으나 기말환율 또한 급락하며 판매보증충당부채전입액 감소 효과가 
나타나 시장 기대를 소폭 상회하는 수익성이 관찰되었다는 것이 26.3Q 실적 총평이다. 
현대차는 2026년 도매 판매 가이던스를 416만대에서 400만대로 하향 조정했다. 
26.3Q 누적 도매 판매 대수는 288만대이기에 하향 조정은 불가피했다. 단 신규 
가이던스에 따르면 26.4Q 판매 목표는 약 110만대 수준이라는 것인데, 아반떼, 투싼, 
주요 모델 페이스리프트, GV80 HEV 등의 신차 효과가 얼마나 나타날지가 관건일 
것이다 (이하 분기 도매 판매, 25.1Q 100.1만대, 25.2Q 105.7만대, 25.3Q 103.8만대).

### Page 2

현대차 (005380)
2
미래 사업 업데이트: 자율주행은 계획대로, Atlas는 조금씩 변신중
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 특별히 SDV~Boston Dynamics 관련 업데이트는 확인되지
않았으나 여러 매체를 통해 파악된 현대차그룹의 미래 사업 진행 상황은 다음과 같다.
① SDV/자율주행: 데이터 플라이 휠에 대한 중요성 강조. 27년 말부터 광주
권역 자율주행 실증 사업에 투입되는 차량은 Atria AI 활용. SDV 플랫폼이
적용된 차량 양산 시작은 29년. 양산 SDV 출하량 증가함에 따라 29년부터
GPU 5만개 이상으로 구성되는 새만금 데이터센터를 자체 운영을 목표함.
박민우 포티투닷 사장 겸 AVP 본부장이 부임한 이후 Tesla, nVIDIA 출신의
개발 인력을 충원 중인 상황. 26.05 그랜저 페이스리프트 모델에 플레오스
탑재 시작. 26.09부터 판매되고 있는 신형 아반떼에도 플레오스 탑재 완료.

② Boston Dynamics: 10.8 Rohit Prasad를 CEO로 선임. Rohit Prasad은
아마존에서 Alex 개발에 중추적인 역할을 수행했던 인물. 현대차그룹에서
직접 하드웨어 개발 및 양산을 챙기고, 소프트웨어에 역량을 높이겠다는
행보로 판단. 26.10 Atlas의 신규 덱스트러스를 공개했는데, 기존 제품 대비
자유도가 크게 증가 (7DoF→14DoF). 순수 직구동 방식을 여전히
고수함.10.27 Boston Dynamics가 주요 부품 업체에게 Atlas 1만대 물량의
부품 PO를 곧 발송할 것이라는 기사가 보도됨. 28년까지 연 3만대 CAPA의
공장 완공이라는 기존 계획 대비 양산 및 개발 속도가 빨라진다는 시그널임.
현대차그룹의 미래 사업 업데이트를 또 찾아볼 수 있을 가장 빠른 이벤트는 CES
2027이다. 다만 CES 2027에는 현대차가 아닌 그룹사가 참여함에 따라 해당 행사에서

유의미한 업데이트 내용은 찾아보기 힘들 것이며 이에 중장기 관점의 접근이 필요하다.
시장의 우려가 반영된 주가 하락: 빨라질 속도와 올라올 본업에 주목
현대차 주가는 26.2H 접어들며 크게 하락하고 있고, CES 2026 전후의 상승폭을 사실상
모두 반납했다 (연초 기준 -1.3%, 26.2H 이후 -41.7%). 또한 최근 시장 반등
흐름에서도 사실상 소외되고 있다. 현대차를 바라보는 투자자들의 우려는 아래와 같다.
첫째, CID 전후로 Boston Dynamics 관련 업데이트가 너무 부족하다는 것이다. 26.08
현대차 CID 행사를 앞두고 많은 투자자들은 빅테크 업체들의 지분 투자 및 Atlas
개발과 관련해 새로운 내용을 기대한 것이 사실이다. 이런 상황에서 유럽, 내수 등 주요
지역 에서의 판매 업황 또한 좋지 않다 보니, 시장의 실망은 더욱 컸다. 전술한 것과
같이 CES 2027에 현대차가 참여하지 않는다는 점에서 내년에 대한 기대도 크지 않다.

둘째, 내수와 유럽 업황이 그룹사 대비 너무 좋지 못하다는 점이다. 26.3Q 현대차 내수,
유럽 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 각 27.2%, 18.1% 감소했다. 반면 26.3Q 기아 내수,
유럽 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 각 2.2%, 18.7% 증가했다. 특히 유럽에서의 부진은
EV 라인업이 상대적으로 빈약하고, 중국 업체와의 경쟁이 심화되고 있다는 구조적
문제에서 출발하기에, 투자자들은 26년을 넘어 27, 28년의 성장까지 우려하고 있다
단 우리는 Boston Dynamics의 Atlas는 양산이라는 방향성이 설정된지 오래되지
않았다는 점을 기억해야 한다. 초기 단계부터 양산을 위해 개발된 Tesla Optimus 대비
개발 속도가 빠를 수는 없다. 1~2만대 수준의 데모 제품이 확보되고, 이를 바탕으로
데이터 수집이 일정 부분 확보되는 이후부터 관련 사업 확대 속도는 빨라 질것이다.

취약 지역 판매 업황의 경우에도 내수를 시작으로 점진적으로 개선될 것이다. 생산 차질
외에도 Tesla 및 중국 OEM들의 시장 침투가 우려됨은 분명하지만, 주력 차종 중에서도
국내 판매 볼륨이 큰 아반떼와 투싼의 신차 효과는 분명 기대되는 지점이다. 유럽에서의
반등은 시간이 필요할 것이나, 일단 아이오닉 3가 26.4Q부터 본격적으로 판매될
예정이다. 단 이는 큰 변동 없이 IAA가 2028년부터 시행된다는 전제에 근거하고 있다.
현대차 목표주가 600,000원 유지: 여전한 Multiple 할증 요인
현대차 목표주가를 600,000원으로 유지한다. 26년 EPS 기준 6~8배 수준의 P/E
Multiple을 부여받고 있는 Peer Group들은 중국 시장 M/S 축소로 수익성이 급락하고
있고, 미래 사업 경쟁력은 여전히 전무하다. 현대차의 Multiple 할증 요인은 충분하다.

그림17. 유럽 생산 OEM 생산원가 추이 
 `  
자료: iM증권 리서치본부

IAA는 EU판 IRA로 볼 수 있음. 현대차그룹의 경우 현대차 (터키, 체코)와 기아 (슬로바키아) 모두 유렵 현지
생산 라인을 이미 확보했고, 현대모비스를 중심으로 한 부품 자급률 비중 이미 높음. 또한 주요 부품에 있어
FTA 체결국의 원산지를 EU산과 동일하게 적용하는 규정 존재. 실제 발효까지 큰 변수가 없다면 국내 완성차
업체의 경우 구조적으로 수혜 클 것. 단 중국 업체들에 대한 명시적 배제 규정이 없다는 점은 한계 중 하나. 
IAA 주요 조항 주요 내용
적용 대상
공공조달 차량, 공적 지원(구매 보조금 등)을 받는 차량, 법인차가 대상임. 전기
차, PHEV, 수소차에 적용됨. 2028년부터 회원국은 무공해 및 저공해이면서 EU
산인 차량이 아니면 법인차를 지원할 수 없음. 판매 금지가 아니라 수요 측 혜택
을 역내 생산과 연동하는 구조
역내 부품 요건
1단계(발효 6개월 후)는 EU 조립, 배터리 제외 부품가치 70% EU산, 셀 포함
배터리 핵심부품 3개 EU산을 요구함. 2단계(1단계 3년 후)는 배터리 핵심부품
5개(셀, 양극재,BMS 포함)와 전동 파워트레인·전장 가치 50% EU산이 추가됨.
소형차 특례
전장 4.2m 이하 전기 승용차가 대상임. EU 조립에 더해 부품가치 70%와
배터리 핵심부품 3개 중 하나만 충족하면 됨. 충족 시 CO2 규제상 1.3대로 인정
됨(2025~34년).
FDI 조건 1억 유로 초과 투자가 대상이며, 지분 49% 상한, EU 인력 50% 이상, EU산 투 입재 30% 이상 등의 조건이 붙음.
FTA·관세동맹국 예외
FTA나 관세동맹을 맺은 국가의 부품은 EU산과 동등한 부품으로 인정되며,
정부조달협정(GPA) 가입국도 일부 조항에 포함됨. 단 최종 조립국까지 인정되
는지는 불명확하며, 조문상으로는 EU, EEA 역내 조립만 인정되는 것으로 해석
됨. 따라서 한국산 부품은 EU산으로 인정 가능하나, 한국이나 튀르키예에서 조
립한 완성차는 요건 미충족 가능성이 높음.
FTA 예외 배제 조건
집행위는 위임법으로 특정국을 동등 대상에서 제외할 수 있음. 해당국이 EU 기업
에 내국민 대우를 하지 않는 경우, 공급망 의존도나 공급 안보 위협이 있는 경우,
해당 협정의 예외 조항에 해당하는 경우가 배제 사유임. 중국은 FTA가 없어 애초
에 대상이 아님.
2020 2021 2022 2023
대당 생산원가 30,683 32,029 35,079 38,140
대당 판매가격 34,404 27,557 31,244 33,560
1인당 인건비 70,981 74,904 78,348 88,198
통상적으로 중국 OEM의 생산 원가 (중국 생산 가정)은 글로벌 OEM 대비 30% 낮다고 추정됨. BYD의 경우 돌핀 등과 같
은 C 세그먼트 차량에서의 유럽 수익성이 낮지만, A~B 세그먼트 급의 돌핀 서프의 경우 매우 높은 수익성 시현. 이는 유럽
시장 내의 A~B 세그먼트 경쟁 강도가 낮다는 점에서 기인. 즉 100% 수준의 관세가 부과되지 않는다면 BYD의 가격 경쟁
력은 유지되는 것. 따라서 EU는 2,000유로 수준의 중국 OEM의 대당 이익을 상쇄하기 위해 이에 준하는 수준으로 지급되
는 EV 구매 보조금 및 법인 차량 세제혜택을 검토 중인 것이고, 이를 IAA에 담아낼지가 핵심.
유럽 생산 OEM 생산원가 및 판매가격 추이

그림23. 26.3Q 국내 완성차 실적 Summary

자료: iM증권 리서치본부

현대차 미주: 펠리세이드 리콜 이슈 존재하나, 메타플랜트 및 기존 앨러배마 공
장 HEV 생산을 통한 시장 진출 속도 빨라질 것으로 전망
인도: GM 탈레가온 공장 인수를 통한 생산량 확대에 나설 예정이고, 이
를 통한 인도 내수 수요 대응 및 글로벌 신흥 시장 진출 또한 목표
유럽: 기아 대비 EV 라인업의 폭에 열위에 있으나, 캐스퍼 EV, EV 3를
통해 EV 수요 회복 국면을 앞두고 있는 유럽 시장 대응에 나설 예정
기아 미주: 텔룰라이드 HEV 출시를 통해 Mid-Size  HEV 수요 대응 이뤄지

China’s October domestic activity data disappoints across the

Asia Outlook 2027: Growth broadens, capital gets selective
Korea: BOK expected to hike rates by 25bp
The Bank of Korea is expected to raise its policy rate by 25bp to
3.25% on Wednesday, marking the third hike of the current
tightening cycle. Inﬂation remains above target, with both headline
and core CPI holding at 2.8% year-on-year in October, while robust
semiconductor exports continue to underpin growth. At the same

### Page 2

time, a moderation in household credit growth suggests earlier rate
hikes are beginning to tighten ﬁnancial conditions.
China: Industrial proﬁts recovery remains
uneven
China will release October industrial proﬁts on Friday. While proﬁts
have generally recovered over 2026, the rebound remains uneven.
Gains have been concentrated in AI- and electronics-related
industries and upstream commodity producers, while automakers,
construction-linked sectors, and many consumer-facing

manufacturers continue to face signiﬁcant pressure. Soft domestic
demand is likely to keep these sectors under pressure in the
October data.
Taiwan: Technology supports industrial
production growth
Taiwan will release October industrial production data. We expect
output growth to accelerate to 22.0% YoY, driven primarily by
continued strength in the technology sector. Outside of tech,
however, activity is likely to remain more subdued. A strong reading

would reinforce expectations for solid economic growth in the
fourth quarter.
Japan: Tokyo inﬂation expected to
strengthen
Japan will release November Tokyo CPI data on Thursday.
Consensus forecasts headline inﬂation to accelerate to 3.1% YoY
from 2.8% in October, while core inﬂation is expected to rise to 2.9%
YoY from 2.6%. A broadly in-line reading would suggest underlying
price pressures remain ﬁrm and reinforce our expectations for a
Bank of Japan rate hike in December.

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -32.53 điểm tương ứng -1.83%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Linh kiện ô tô, Dầu khí và Công nghệ là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -634 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VPL; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-609 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -5,829 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -1,462 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 722 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -5,278 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam đánh mất đà hồi phục của tuần trước, khi VN-Index quay đầu giảm khoảng 
1,8% về đóng cửa quanh 1.742 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm lùi sâu về sát 1.727 
điểm trước khi thu hẹp đà giảm. Dù mùa báo cáo KQKD quý 3 diễn ra tương đối tích cực, 
tuy nhiên các lo ngại về việc môi trường lãi suất cao vẫn duy trì trong bối cảnh căng thẳng 
Trung Đông leo thang đang đè nặng lên tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Kết quả kỳ họp của FED trong 
tuần qua nhìn chung phù hợp với kỳ vọng và không gây ra ảnh hưởng đáng kể. Điểm kém 
tích cực nhất là khối ngoại đã quay lại bán ròng với tổng giá trị khoảng 2.700 tỷ đồng 
trong tuần — đảo ngược diễn biến mua ròng cuối tuần trước, cho thấy đà rút vốn ngoại 
vẫn chưa thực sự dừng lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến giảm trên đồ thị tuần, nhưng chưa thoái lui quá 1 
nửa cây nến tăng của tuần trước, cùng thanh khoản được tiết giảm, hàm ý nhịp điều chỉnh 
vẫn trong phạm vi cho phép. Trên khung ngày, sau bốn phiên giằng co đầu tuần với thanh 
khoản thấp, chỉ số đã chịu áp lực sụt giảm mạnh hơn trong phiên cuối tuần, chớm xuống 
dưới đường tín hiệu chính giữa dải Bollinger, nhưng cho tín hiệu rút chân ngay sau đó, thể 
hiện áp lực bán tháo đã tạm thời được kiểm soát. Mặc dù rủi ro mở rộng thêm nhịp điều 
chỉnh vẫn còn để ngỏ, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX 
được kỳ vọng sẽ cho phản ứng hồi phục trở lại tại các điểm đỡ bên dưới.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua.

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Consumers Feel One Way, but Spend Another
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
Americans have spent much of the past several years telling surveys they are cautious
about the economy. Yet they continue to spend as though they are not. The University
of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index remains near multidecade lows, while the
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence has also been in a yearslong downtrend.

These surveys reflect a list of persistent concerns from elevated food and gas prices to
geopolitical uncertainty and labor market anxiety.
Even so, consumer spending has remained remarkably resilient. The final estimate of Q2
real consumer spending, as measured by PCE, was revised up to an annualized rate of
3.8%, well above the 2.3% 20-year average. More recent data suggest that momentum
has continued. According to Bank of America Institute, the latest monthly total credit and

debit card spending data per household rose 6.2% year-over-year (YoY) in September, and
spending excluding gasoline was up 5.1% YoY, both the third strongest growth rates in the
past four years.1
The persistence of consumer spending is particularly notable given that temporary cyclical
boosts like larger tax refunds are starting to fade. That raises an important question: What is
actually funding strong consumption today? Recent data suggest the drivers are becoming

increasingly more diversified, with broadening labor market trends, healthy consumer
finances and elevated wealth effects all reinforcing one another and underpinning consumers.
First, the labor market continues to provide an important foundation for consumer spending,
and recent trends suggest wage growth is supporting a broader set of households than in
recent years. Jobs growth has slowed from the exceptionally strong pace seen following the
pandemic, but broader indicators suggest labor market conditions remain relatively stable.

Although the September jobs report came in softer than expected, the three- and six-month
rolling averages indicate that the trend in jobs growth likely bottomed out last year and has
gradually improved since (Exhibit 1A). Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remains low by
historical standards and layoffs remain contained. And while wage growth has moderated
and recently trailed headline inflation (Exhibit 1B), cumulative income gains over the past
several years have likely helped many households absorb higher prices better.

An important development though has been improving wage growth trends among lower-
income households, where spending tends to be most sensitive to changes in wages. Bank
of America Institute consumer deposit account data show after-tax wage growth for lower-
income households outpaced higher-income households for a second consecutive month
in August, rising 4.7% YoY compared with 3.5% for higher-income households. Importantly,
this improvement appears to be supporting discretionary spending, not just spending

on necessities. Lower-income discretionary spending rose 5.7% YoY in August, nearly
matching the 5.9% pace for higher-income households. In other words, the narrowing gap
in wage and spending growth across income cohorts suggests consumer resilience looks
to be becoming less dependent on affluent households alone, creating a broader and more
durable foundation for consumption.
Second, household finances remain in relatively good shape, providing consumers with the

flexibility to continue spending. The ratio of household debt to disposable personal income
remains near its lowest level since the late 1990s, excluding the temporary distortions
caused by pandemic-related income support in 2020 and 2021 (Exhibit 1C). Recent
revisions to the Bureau of Economic Analysis national income accounts also suggest
consumers have a larger financial buffer than previous data suggested. Personal saving
estimates over the past year have been revised higher by $320 billion, or 39%, and the

revised savings rate remains above 4% after previous estimates showed it fell to 3% during
the summer.2 Concerns that consumers may be rapidly exhausting their savings buffers
may therefore be overstated. Supporting this view, Bank of America Institute consumer
1 Bank of America Institute, “Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers.” As of October 9, 2026.
2 Bank of America, “Massive Savings Markup = Another Reason to Be Bullish.” As of October 2, 2026.

Investment Implications
Consumer spending strength
remains a key pillar of economic
and earnings growth, which keeps
us constructive on our Equity
overweight. The growing connection
between household wealth and
spending reinforces the importance
of maintaining a diversified portfolio.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
deposit account data show that middle- and lower-income households continue to maintain
deposit balances above prepandemic levels across age groups.
Third, elevated household wealth continues to provide a powerful tailwind for consumer
spending, particularly among households that account for an outsized share of overall
consumption. U.S. household net worth rose $12.8 trillion (T) to a record $195.9T in Q2,

driven largely by gains in Equities, which contributed $10.7T of the increase (Exhibit 1D).
As a result, household net worth relative to disposable income climbed to a record 8.3,
meaning households now hold more than eight dollars of wealth for every dollar of annual
disposable income. Households are also sitting on $35.8T in home equity, an all-time high
level that provides a significant balance sheet cushion. Rising asset values have helped to

support spending through what economists refer to as wealth effects. In other words, when
households feel wealthier, they tend to be more willing to spend.
For investors, the key takeaway is that consumer spending is being supported by multiple
engines despite the weaker sentiment data. Labor income trends are supporting wider
participation, healthy balance sheets are helping households absorb economic pressures,
and elevated household wealth continues to sustain higher-income spending. Together,

these forces are supportive of economic growth and corporate profits, which is particularly
important since the consumer accounts for roughly 70% of U.S. gross domestic product.
That said, it does not eliminate risk. A meaningful risk to the consumer would be a scenario
in which both the labor and wealth engines weaken simultaneously. For now, labor market
conditions remain stable, and wealth effects remain supportive. Equity market pullbacks in
recent years have done little to derail consumer spending, suggesting it would likely take a

deeper and more prolonged drawdown to alter consumer behavior.
Exhibit 1: Consumer Spending is Being Supported by Multiple Engines.
A) Jobs growth has stablized. B) Wage growth have helped offset higher prices.

4 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
The Bull Market Turns Four: Older, Unloved—But Still Kicking
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Four years ago this month, virtually no one was in the mood to buy U.S. Equities, and for
good reasons.
Rewind to October 2022: Inflation the month before clocked in at an annual rate of 8.2%.

The yield curve was inverted, a signal that has preceded most past recessions. Consumer
sentiment was abysmal, having dropped to a record low in June 2022. Recession
concerns were mounting, with an October Wall Street Journal poll survey putting the
odds of a recession within 12 months at about 63%. And realizing (belatedly) that
inflation was not “transitory,” the Fed was in the midst of administering one of the most
aggressive monetary tightening cycles in decades, raising the fed funds rate five times

between March and September 2022 by a stunning 300 basis points.
Reflecting the mood at the moment, the S&P 500 closed at 3,577 on October 12, 2022,
down 25% below its January peak, leaving investors battered and bruised but blind to the
fact that the market had bottomed. Four years on, the bull market that emerged from
the gloom of 2022 is still standing and kicking, with the S&P 500 up roughly 130% since
the October 2022 lows.
The more than doubling of the S&P over the past four years has hardly been linear—and

has been accompanied with plenty of pain and doubt. Exhibit 2 maps the rocky path
trodden by the bull over the past four years. Less than six months into the rally, the
markets were rocked by the failure of Silicon Valley Bank (SVB) on March 10, 2023. The
S&P Regional Banks Select Industry Index lost 18% that week and stoked concerns of
massive deposit runs and credit stress across the capital markets. However, owing to
emergency intervention by the government, lower yields and strength elsewhere in the

markets, the crisis passed. Some banks failed that month, but the bull market didn’t.
Exhibit 2: Revisiting the Rocky Path Trodden by the Bull.
Period "Reason to Sell" What happened instead
October 2022 Inflation >8%, aggressive Fed tightening, recession concerns Bull market begins
March 2023 SVB/regional banking crisis Stocks keep climbing
2023-24 Inverted yield curve, “recession inevitable” Economy keeps expanding
2024 “Magnificent 7* bubble,” narrow breadth, expensive valuations Earnings propel market higher

2025 Tariffs, trade war, policy uncertainty Market absorbs another shock
2026 Oil/diesel shock, 5%+ yields, Fed tightening, geopolitical risks Bull reaches fourth birthday
*Apple, Amazon, Alphabet, Nvidia, Meta, Microsoft and Tesla. Source: Chief Investment Office. As of October 9, 2026.
But recessionary concerns hung like threatening clouds over the markets for most of
2023. Aggressive Fed tightening, an inverted yield curve, and soft economic data were

all taken as harbingers of an economic slowdown and profits recession. Yet the S&P 500
continued to climb in 2023, up 26.3% for the year, driven in large part by the outsized
price performance of the Magnificent Seven.
Investors spent much of 2024 fretting about a market too dependent on technology,
and therefore a market too narrow and too expensive. The unwinding of the Japan carry
trade in August 2024 engendered more angst, as did uncertainties around the 2024

U.S. presidential election. But despite a host of misgivings, the S&P 500 delivered total
returns of 25% in 2024. The unloved bull marched on.
Total returns in 2025 were robust as well—roughly 18%—notwithstanding—yet
again—a number of headwinds. In late January, DeepSeek, a cheaper China AI model,
raised questions about America’s technology leadership and the need to spend enormous
sums on chips and data centers. The Nasdaq promptly dropped by 3.1% thereafter.

Investment Implications
The adoption phase of the AI buildout
should translate to broader earnings
growth and performance across
sectors, reinforcing our conviction
in U.S. Equities. Given a strong
fundamental backdrop, we would
continue to buy on weakness as the
bull enters its fifth year

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
January’s jolt was followed by “Liberation Day,” or the warning of punishing U.S. tariffs
on America’s major trading partners. The trade-policy shock of April 2025 caused the
S&P 500 to drop nearly 19% from its February high to its April 8 low on uncertainty
that higher import costs would decrease corporate margins and consumer spending and
upend global supply chains. Amid the trade tumult, Treasury prices fell and the U.S. dollar

weakened on concerns that foreigners were abandoning U.S. assets. Remember all the
angst over the dollar debasement trade? If that wasn’t enough, expensive valuations and
enormous capital commitments raised doubts about the AI trade and triggered talk of an
AI bubble. Yet despite this wall of worry, the S&P 500, for the third year running, posted
double-digit gains in 2025.
And double-digit gains could be in store this year, with the S&P 500 up 14% through

October 9. The bull has rumbled on, taking in stride the energy shock from the war in
the Middle East, the shift in interest rate expectations from the Fed (from rate cuts
to rate hikes), and the rising uncertainty associated with the midterm elections. These
headwinds have been countered by tailwinds such as better-than-expected corporate
earnings and better-than-anticipated real growth.
Finally, as Exhibit 3 makes clear, what has given this bull so much muscle has been

the U.S. Technology sector, specifically the Magnificent 7. Powering the bull has been
the Magnificent 7, with a staggering 344% return since the 2022 lows, while U.S.
technology has delivered a 277% return versus the S&P 500’s return of 130%. Note the
outperformance of the S&P 500 relative to the main international indices.
Looking forward: Is the bull’s greatest strength also its Achilles’ heel? Bull
markets don’t die of natural causes. And they typically don’t expire because of one

particular signal or indicator. Bull markets turn to bear markets when a cluster of signals/
metrics turn south. Think deteriorating and downward earnings revisions, higher interest
rates, widening credit spreads and tighter lending standards, growth disappointments,
stretched valuations and sentiment, deteriorating market breadth, and an external
shock. Any one of these signals could flash red and not trouble the bull.  But when they
converge or feed off each other, that’s trouble.

In addition, since a great deal of the earnings growth, market leadership and capital
expenditures (capex) spending of this bull market have come from the technology sector,
the bull’s greatest strength could become its greatest vulnerability. AI has become an
investment ecosystem unto itself, and hence the fifth year of the bull market will pivot
in part around AI revenue, frontier model adoption rates, infrastructure spending, free
cash flows, depreciation costs, debt issuance, data center utilization, cloud growth, and

competition via cheaper models and computing alternatives. To put it mildly, there is a
great deal at stake when it comes to the bull and AI.
However, this is where we stand today: We believe the bull will continue to rumble
ahead next year, with AI revenues and productivity gains justifying the costs of the AI
infrastructure. Critically, AI is moving from the buildout phase, dominated by chipmakers
and hyperscaler capex, into the adoption phase, where software firms, healthcare,

finance, energy and a myriad of other firms start to see productivity gains and margin
expansion from rising AI adoption rates. We believe this will give better breadth to the
S&P 500; so too will continued profit growth across various U.S. sectors, AI investment
that starts to pay off, and an economy capable of absorbing setbacks or periodic bouts
of uncertainty.
The path forward won’t be linear—and there will be fits and starts, and market
drawdowns. That’s normal for any market. But that aside, the bull will keep rumbling,

roaming and kicking in 2027.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What is our view of the macro and market landscape heading
into 2027 and beyond?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
A supply side-led economic expansion continues, driven by capital investment in the AI
buildout, solid consumer spending and fiscal tailwinds still blowing, albeit with less force.
This creates a backdrop that keeps nominal growth well-above trend and, importantly,

above the interest cost on national debt.
The interest rate backdrop largely remains the same as of today, but, instead of the
sharp increases that we experienced in 2026, the curve should settle down, and yields
should remain relatively range bound. The Fed is likely to tap on the breaks a couple of
more times before reassessing new inflation, growth and employment trends later in
2027.
Concerns surrounding credit risk and "circular" financing are likely to rise the further we

move into the AI buildout, but we do not expect a default cycle to develop. Rather, we
expect the sharp backup in yields to place pressure namely on lower-quality credits in
areas having a difficult time building the cash flow necessary to pay the higher interest
costs.
We expect a broadening out within the market but technology to still maintain its
heavyweight positioning. As we move from training and evolving the large language
models to agentic, and physical AI new leaders are likely to develop. In addition, we

anticipate AI adopters such as biotechnology, transport, logistics, advance manufacturing
and financial companies to enter new growth and productivity phases.
Lastly, overall earnings growth in the U.S. should be low to mid double-digit percent
stages in 2027, which is about half of the growth from 2026 thus far but still around
double from normal levels.
Credit risks developing and higher energy prices for longer are the main risks to our bull
case for Equities extending further into 2027 and beyond.

Index Definitions
ecurities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

University of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index is a monthly survey of consumer confidence levels in the United States conducted by the University of Michigan.
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence measures what consumers are feeling about their expected financial situation, whether that's optimistic or pessimistic.
Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and

services.
Nasdaq Composite Index is a stock market measure that tracks the performance of more than 3,000 stocks listed on the Nasdaq exchange.
MSCI Emerging Markets c is a stock market index that tracks the performance of large-cap and mid-cap companies across 24 developing and fast-growing nations.
MSCI All-Country World Index ex USA is a market-capitalization-weighted stock market index that tracks large- and mid-cap equity performance across developed and emerging markets

worldwide, excluding the United States.
MSCI Europe, Australasia, and the Far East (EAFE) Index is a stock market index that measures the performance of large- and mid-cap companies across 21 developed.
Dow Jones Industrial Average Index is a stock market index that tracks 30 major public companies listed on the New York Stock Exchange (NYSE) and Nasdaq.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is an alternative version of the standard S&P 500 that gives every constituent company an identical weight of 0.2% during each quarterly rebalance, rather

than weighting companies by their market capitalization.
Russell 2000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of approximately 2,000 small-cap (small-capitalization) public companies in the United States.
S&P 500 sub-sectors and industry groups Global Industry Classification Standard (GICS®)/S&P 500 Total Return Index, including Information Technology Total Return (TR) USD;
Consumer Discretionary TR USD; Industrials TR USD; Real Estate TR USD; Communication Services TR USD; Materials  TR USD; Financials TR USD; Consumer Staples TR USD; Utilities TR USD;

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

### Page 1

张涛在北京⼤学演讲指出， AI 应⽤创业应⾯向 6 个⽉后的模型外溢能⼒设计产品，⽽⾮修补
当下的模型能⼒； 1 秒延迟的视频⽣成模型出现时，可能成为新⼀代操作系统。他还提醒，
免费补贴是 AI 时代最⼤的陷阱，它制造⽆效反馈、扭曲产品判断，让⽤⼾真实感受到智能
才是 AI 产品的价值。 “ 我们被上⼀代移动互联⽹创业的免费补贴陷阱给迷惑了。 ”
摘要：
近⽇， Manus 联合创始⼈、⾸席产品官张涛在北京⼤学 AI 创投课堂发表了⼀场⻓达两⼩时的演
讲，披露了这家 30 ⼈公司从 0 到 1 的创业决策链条。在这场演讲中，张涛抛出了⼀个在当前 AI 创
业圈颇具争议的判断：免费补贴是 AI 应⽤创业最⼤的陷阱，中国 AI 创业者可能在被上⼀代移动互
联⽹的打法所误导。
张涛在演讲中透露， Manus 发布后第⼀个⽉的 ARR （年度经常性收⼊）即突破 4000 万元⼈⺠
币，接近 5000 万，核⼼⽤⼾中不乏每⽉在平台消费 4000 ⾄ 5000 美元的企业管理者。他将这⼀成
绩归结于⼀个核⼼判断：在 AI 时代，让⽤⼾真实感受到智能，才是产品最重要的价值，⽽⾮靠补

贴堆砌 DAU 。
砍掉 7 个⽉⼼⾎：⼀个关于时间成本的战略判断
在 Manus 诞⽣之前，有⼀段鲜为⼈知的弯路。
2024 年 3 ⽉，张涛与联合创始⼈、⾸席科学家 Pick 加⼊肖弘（ Red ）的团队后，团队的第⼀个⽅
向并⾮ Agent ，⽽是⼀款 AI 原⽣浏览器。逻辑顺理成章：公司此前的产品 Monica 是浏览器插件，
天花板明显， " 做浏览器插件出⾝的公司，很容易推演到下⼀个产品应该做个浏览器 " 。
这款浏览器做了整整 7 个⽉，到 2024 年 10 ⽉已接近发布状态。张涛坦⾔， " 发出来应该对市场还
是有点惊艳的 " 。但肖弘最终拍板将其 sunset （放弃）。
我觉得回看过去两三年，中国 AI 应⽤创业，我觉得有⼀个⾮常⼤的陷阱，就是我们被上⼀代移动
互联⽹创业的免费补贴陷阱给迷惑了，以为这⼀代还要这么做，完全错误。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

核⼼原因有两个。其⼀，产品体验的嫁接困境：所有⼈拿到 AI 浏览器后，第⼀个问题都是 " 我在
Chrome ⾥⽤的功能，在你这⾥去哪找 " ，⽤⼾永远⽤旧体验的基准线来衡量新产品，⽽创业团队
根本⽆⼒在浏览器基础功能上对⻬苹果和 Google 。其⼆，时间成本的判断：⼀旦发布，团队将
被迫在这个⽅向上耗费 3 ⾄ 6 个⽉，⽽ " 如果你在创业，钱是最不值钱的东西，浪费了时间才是最
可怕的 " 。
张涛事后评价，这是 Manus 历史上最重要的⼀个决定， " 如果当时不是因为 sunset 了那个浏览器
产品，就没有后来的 Manus" 。
看⻅拐点：从 Cursor ⾥读出的不是 Coding 机会
2024 年 7 ⽉， Cursor 爆⽕。市场上随即涌现出⼤量 Coding Agent 的跟随者。张涛和 Pick 看到的却
是另⼀件事。
" 我们发现 Sonnet 3.5 V2 解锁的不是单纯的 Coding 能⼒，⽽是⼀个 agentic 能⼒。 " 张涛说，这包
括两个维度： Long Horizon Planning （⻓程规划） —— 模型开始主动将任务拆解为多步骤，⽽

⾮在单轮对话中强⾏完成；以及⼤幅增强的 Tool Use （⼯具调⽤）能⼒。
在张涛看来，这个能⼒解锁的对象不是⼯程师，⽽是普通⼈。 " 这个世界不需要更多的 engineer
了，⽽且 engineer 也不需要更多 fancy 的⼯具了。 " 他们看到的机会是：通过 Coding 作为中介，去
解决普通⽤⼾的通⽤场景问题。
2024 年 12 ⽉， Manus 正式⽴项。两个半⽉后， 2025 年 3 ⽉ 5 ⽇发布。发布当天，张涛才发
现， " 好像全世界只有我们在做这个，⼤家都还是在做 Coding" 。
这背后有⼀套 Pick ⼀直坚持的架构哲学，张涛将其概括为 "Less structure, more intelligence" ：
Manus 的 Honeycomb 架构从第⼀版起就完全抛弃了 workflow ，每⼀步的思考和决策全部交由模
型⾃主决定， " 不管你让 Manus 做 research 、做 slides 、做⽹站、写数据分析，所有的事情都是
Manus ⾃⼰决定下⼀步⼲什么的 " 。

这个赌注在 2025 年 2 ⽉中旬 Anthropic 发布 Claude 3.7 的当晚得到验证。张涛在即刻发⽂ " 信仰充
值的⼀晚 "——Honeycomb ⼀⾏代码未改，仅将模型 endpoint 从 3.5 V2 切换⾄ 3.7 ， benchmark 上
剩余未解决的问题 " 百分之⼆三⼗，全部⼀下就解了 " 。
反对 “ 互联⽹式烧钱 ” ：免费补贴是 AI 创业最⼤的陷阱
Manus 发布时，连付费功能都没有做。3 ⽉ 5 ⽇上线，发现⽕爆程度超出预期，团队才从零开始
搭建定价体系，到 3 ⽉ 20 多号才上线付费功能。
⽽在这中间，邀请码成了救命稻草。原因极为现实：公司账上只剩⼋九百万美元，在有邀请码
限制的情况下，单⽇最⾼烧掉 50 万美元。 " 如果没有邀请码，我们公司从 3 ⽉ 5 号发布，可能就⼀
撑⼀个星期， 3 ⽉ 12 号就可以关⻔了。 "
但张涛想说的不只是这段险情，⽽是⼀个更根本的判断：AI 时代，免费补贴是错误⽅向。
我觉得回看过去两三年，中国 AI 应⽤创业有⼀个⾮常⼤的陷阱，就是我们被上⼀代移动互联⽹创

业的免费补贴陷阱给迷惑了，以为这⼀代还要这么做，完全错误。
为什么？因为 AI 这个时代，智能本⾝，如果说你的 performance 本⾝是⾜够好的，⼀定会有⼈
愿意付费的，⼀定是有⼈愿意付费的。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
他的逻辑是：免费补贴吸引来的是⽆效⽤⼾，这些⽤⼾的反馈会迷惑创业者的注意⼒；⽽付费
⽤⼾是⽤钱投票的⽤⼾，他们的需求才是真实需求。 " 你围绕那个需求去做，你就能吸引更多愿
意付费的⼈。 "
他还特别点出⼤⼚的困境：上线第⼀天拿⼀两千万预算补贴⽤⼾，有了⼀⼆⼗万 DAU 之后，没
有⼈敢在第⼆个⽉转向收费， " 它就⼀直拖累了你对于真实⽤⼾市场的感知能⼒ " 。
Manus ⽬前最满意且付费最多的⽤⼾群体，是企业管理者 ——CEO 、⼩⽼板、部⻔主管。张涛
的解释直接： "General AI Agent ， General 通⽤，在公司⾥⾯谁的屁事最多？⽼板嘛。 "
下⼀个转折点在哪⾥：⾯向 “6 个⽉后的模型 ”
张涛在演讲中给出了⼀个具体的创业⽅向预判，他称之为 "1 秒钟延迟的 Sora 时刻 " 。
" 我⼀直在等待⼀个时刻，就是从你输出 prompt 到你看到画⾯延迟⼩于 1 秒钟。我觉得当这个
turning point 到来的时候，会是这⼀轮 AI 整个应⽤创业⾮常重要的转折点。 "

他的判断是， 1 秒延迟的视频⽣成模型已经不是视频⽣成模型，" 它就是新⼀代的操作系统 "。届
时的交互⽅式将从单向内容消费变为每个⼈⾃主参与的互动创作， " 它已经不是单纯的⼀⼈制
作、多⼈消费了 " 。他认为这⼀时刻 "6 个⽉之内⼀定会发⽣ " ，并⿎励创业者现在就开始围绕这个
⽅向准备产品形态。
这⼀判断背后，是张涛对 AI 创业⽅法论的⼀贯主张：永远⾯向 6 个⽉后的模型外溢能⼒去创新，
⽽不是基于今天的模型能⼒修修补补。 " 你应该想象的是 6 个⽉之后模型会变成什么样⼦。如果 6
个⽉之后模型是那个样⼦，我会⽤什么样的产品形态去匹配那个时候的模型。 "
他同时强调，坚持做 General （通⽤）是 Manus 的另⼀个关键战略判断。 " 品牌⼼智的认知是很
难花钱去买的，你在⽤⼾⼼中的 Mindset ，是花⼏亿美⾦都很难改变的事情。 " 正因为⼀开始占住
了 " 什么都能⼲ " 的⼼智， Manus 在后续推出 WebDev 、 Slides 、 Video 等⼦功能时都顺理成章，⽽

那些从垂直场景起家的竞品，想转向通⽤任务时则⾯临⽤⼾认知的强烈阻⼒。
在资本效率问题上，张涛透露， Manus 做到 20 亿美元估值时，历史上总共花掉的融资额约为
5000 ⾄ 6000 万美元。 " 我们团队可能是国内 AI startup ⾥资本利⽤效率最⾼的⼀⽀团队。 " 他的结
论是： AI 创业与其他创业没有本质区别， " 不是因为加了个 AI 的定语，你就可以很理所当然地亏
钱 " 。

### Page 4

市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 398: The AI Trade Is Dead, Long Live The AI Trade!

Source PDF: all report pdf/The AI Trade Is Dead, Long Live The AI Trade!.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: The AI Trade Is Dead, Long Live The AI Trade!
Date:    1 hour ago
The AI Trade Is Dead, Long Live The AI Trade!

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
The AI Trade Is Dead, Long Live
The AI Trade!
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 17 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Frolicsome Fairy / Unsplash

I. The AI Trade Is Dead!
"The King is dead, long live the King!" is a traditional proclamation
announcing a monarch's death while immediately saluting their successor
to show unbroken royal continuity. We think this proclamation applies to
the CEO of Nvidia and the broader AI trade.

### Page 2

Over the past few weeks, we have frequently discussed three key
constraints on the AI investment boom: physical, financial, and political.
Lately, all three have been in play. The AI buildout still needs more
semiconductors and more power. In recent months, the bond vigilantes
have driven up costs for AI borrowers, especially the neoclouds. Then
came the Democrats' midterm sweep, raising fears of growing political
opposition to data center construction.

Now we have to contend with the resignation of Nvidia's CEO Jensen
Huang. The alleged Huang honeytrap scandal adds another layer of
complexity to everyone's outlook for the AI industry.
On the financial front, under Huang, Nvidia has acted as the central bank
of the AI trade, holding $99 billion in equity investments across the AI
ecosystem as of July, plus $25 billion in commitments. It also announced
Wall Street partnerships to mobilize more than $500 billion in third-party

infrastructure financing. But Huang's influence extended beyond these
arrangements. His reputation as the Godfather of AI gave Nvidia-backed
companies a powerful vote of confidence, helping them attract capital from
other investors and lenders. His departure raises questions about both
sources of financial support.
On the political front, the Nvidia-Megaspeed relationship exposed three
potential export-control issues for the US: (1) Intermediaries have been

sending restricted chips to China. (2) China has been accessing overseas
computing capacity remotely. (3) The identities of end-users haven't been
properly vetted. The Commerce Department's latest restrictions aim to
address all three through tighter export controls, remote-access controls,
and due diligence requirements.
Physical constraints could also intensify if China retaliates by restricting
critical mineral exports, potentially disrupting semiconductor production

and the broader AI buildout.
Investors increasingly feared that these constraints could become
roadblocks, bringing the AI investment boom to an abrupt halt.
II. Long Live The AI Trade!

### Page 3

We anticipated buying opportunities in the stock market after the midterm
elections. We see more opportunities than we expected because of the
mounting panic over the AI trade. We view the constraints as speed
bumps that are slowing the AI race. Slowing the pace should reduce the
number of accidents. But the AI investment boom should continue
because the most important variable is demand for compute, which should
keep growing rapidly for the foreseeable future.

Nvidia's latest Q3 earnings and conference call today bolstered our
conviction. Here are our key takeaways:
(1) Another earnings beat. Q3 revenue of $115.8 billion exceeded
expectations of $109.0 billion, while adjusted EPS of $2.68 beat estimates
of $2.47. Data center revenue surged 110% y/y to $107.6 billion,
confirming robust compute demand.
(2) Stronger Q4 outlook. Management guided for Q4 revenue of $130
billion, above the consensus of $125 billion. Nvidia's next-generation Vera

Rubin platform has also begun shipping.
(3) Even stronger FY 2027 guidance. Interim CEO Collette Kress raised
projected revenue growth from 70% to 75%, easing concerns that the
scandal and tighter export restrictions would undermine Nvidia's outlook.
Notably, the forecast assumes no data center compute revenue from
China.
(4) Slowing financing activities. Kress reaffirmed Nvidia's support for
the AI ecosystem while emphasizing more selective neocloud financing.

This will undoubtedly be a speed bump for the neoclouds. We don't expect
a wave of bankruptcies in this space. Instead, slower compute expansion
should boost cloud usage pricing and revenues.
(5) No Jensen comeback. Nvidia's board confirmed that it does not
intend to reinstate Jensen Huang as CEO. Colette Kress remains interim
CEO as the search for a permanent successor continues.
Speed bumps reduce the risk of a boom-bust cycle. They are very useful

when an investment boom risks taking the next corner too fast. They
reduce crash risk.

### Page 4

clause.

The scenario, forecasts and nowcasts of the Economic Research 
– 
23 
Novem
ber 2026

### Page 2

UNITED STATES 
The US economy is expected to grow above its potential in 2026, with an average annual growth rate of 2.2%, close to the 2025 rate (2.1%). 
This apparent resilience to the uncertainty, tariff and energy shocks, together with above-trend productivity growth, masks K-shaped growth, 
driven by investment linked to AI-optimism and consumption by the wealthiest amid a wealth effect (historically high stock market valuations). 
Inflation overshooting is set to continue (3.5% in 2026) at least through 2028, largely because of the rise in oil prices and tariffs – although the 
impact of these latter appears to be less significant than expected. The labour market is showing clear signs of improvement that should bring 
the unemployment rate down toward 4.0%. Given this shift in price and employment risks amid dynamic growth, the FOMC proceeded with a 
first rate hike at September’s meeting and we expect further 25bp increases at the three upcoming meetings (December 2026, January and 
March 2027), bringing the Fed Funds target range at 4.50% - 4.75%.

CHINA 
Economic growth rebounded to 4.5% y/y in Q3 2026 (1.2% q/q) after 4.3% y/y in Q2 and 5.0% in Q1. It is projected to reach 4.6% in 2026 as a 
whole, down from 5% in 2025. Growth remains characterised by a K-shaped trajectory, and the gap between the strong performance of exports 
and the fragility of sectors that rely on domestic demand continues to widen. Retail sales barely increased in the first ten months of 2026, the 
crisis in the property sector continues and household confidence remains low. Yet new fiscal policy measures could help support investment 
and private consumption in the very short term. Deflationary pressures are persisting, given weakness in domestic demand. Energy prices and 
electronic goods prices are increasing, producer price inflation is accelerating (5.7% y/y in October), but consumer price inflation remains low 
(0.7% y/y).

EUROZONE 
The economy has shown resilience in the face of the energy shock: GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3, and the 1.0% growth carry-over for 2026 
supports our forecast of 1.1% for the year as a whole (compared to 1.3% in 2025) with the risk tilted to the upside. Growth is expected to 
strengthen to 1.6% in 2027, bolstered by increased spending on defence and infrastructure, as well as the global investment cycle in AI. Annual 
average inflation is thus expected to reach 3.1% in 2026 (from 2.1% in 2025), and 3.2% in 2027. Given persistent inflation and resilient growth, 
we anticipate another 25bp hike from the ECB in December 2026, bringing the deposit rate to 2.75%, followed by an extended pause. That 
said, stronger than expected second-round effects could lead to more tightening than our current baseline.
